      precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody ? 3 :
      precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody ? 4 :
      precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody ? 13 :
      precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody ? 14 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and open <= close[1] + syminfo.mintick and close > (open[1] + close[1]) * 0.50 and close < open[1] ? 15 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and open >= close[1] - syminfo.mintick and close < (open[1] + close[1]) * 0.50 and close > open[1] ? 16 :
      precedingDownContext[2] and close[2] < open[2] and bodyRatio[2] >= 0.55 and bodyRatio[1] <= 0.30 and math.max(open[1], close[1]) <= (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 + nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * 0.10 and close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.50 and close > (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 ? 17 :
      precedingUpContext[2] and close[2] > open[2] and bodyRatio[2] >= 0.55 and bodyRatio[1] <= 0.30 and math.min(open[1], close[1]) >= (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 - nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * 0.10 and close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.50 and close < (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 ? 18 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and math.max(open, close) <= open[1] and math.min(open, close) >= close[1] and bodyRatio <= bodyRatio[1] * 0.75 ? 19 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and math.max(open, close) <= close[1] and math.min(open, close) >= open[1] and bodyRatio <= bodyRatio[1] * 0.75 ? 20 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and math.abs(low - low[1]) <= math.max(safeAtr * 0.05, syminfo.mintick * 3.0) ? 21 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and math.abs(high - high[1]) <= math.max(safeAtr * 0.05, syminfo.mintick * 3.0) ? 22 :
      high < high[1] and low > low[1] ? 12 :
      bodyRatio > 0.10 and bodyRatio <= 0.30 and upperWick >= realBody and lowerWick >= realBody ? 26 :
      bodyRatio <= 0.45 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody * 1.25 ? 6 :
      bodyRatio <= 0.45 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody * 1.25 ? 7 :
      close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.85 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.08 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.08 ? 8 :
      close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.85 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.08 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.08 ? 9 :
      close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 27 : close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 28 : close > open ? 10 : close < open ? 11 : 0
    code
int candlePatternCode = f_candle_pattern_code()
bool bullishReversalPatternRaw = candlePatternCode == 1 or candlePatternCode == 3 or candlePatternCode == 13 or candlePatternCode == 15 or candlePatternCode == 17 or candlePatternCode == 19 or candlePatternCode == 21 or (candlePatternCode == 23 and precedingDownContext)
bool bearishReversalPatternRaw = candlePatternCode == 2 or candlePatternCode == 4 or candlePatternCode == 14 or candlePatternCode == 16 or candlePatternCode == 18 or candlePatternCode == 20 or candlePatternCode == 22 or (candlePatternCode == 24 and precedingUpContext)
f_price_action_breakouts() =>
    bool insideBar = high < high[1] and low > low[1]
    bool bullInsideSetup = usePriceActionBreakouts and insideBar and precedingDownContext
    bool bearInsideSetup = usePriceActionBreakouts and insideBar and precedingUpContext
    float fastRelativeVolume = ta.ema(relativeVolume, 3)
    float slowRelativeVolume = ta.ema(relativeVolume, 8)
    bool relativeVolumeRising = volumeAvailable and not na(fastRelativeVolume) and not na(slowRelativeVolume) and fastRelativeVolume > slowRelativeVolume and fastRelativeVolume > fastRelativeVolume[1]
    bool volumeAfterDecline = relativeVolumeRising and precedingDownContext and closeLocation >= 0.50
    bool volumeAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult = relativeVolumeRising and precedingUpContext and closeLocation <= 0.50
    float rangeHigh = ta.highest(high[1], sidewaysRangeBars)
    float rangeLow = ta.lowest(low[1], sidewaysRangeBars)
    float rangeWidth = rangeHigh - rangeLow
    bool rangeReady = usePriceActionBreakouts and not na(rangeHigh) and not na(rangeLow) and rangeWidth > syminfo.mintick and rangeWidth <= nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * sidewaysRangeAtrWidth
    bool declineBeforeRange = rangeReady and precedingDownContext[sidewaysRangeBars]
    bool advanceBeforeRange = rangeReady and precedingUpContext[sidewaysRangeBars]
    bool bullInsideBreakRaw = usePriceActionBreakouts and barstate.isconfirmed and bullInsideSetup[1] and close > high[2] + breakBuffer
    bool bearInsideBreakRaw = usePriceActionBreakouts and barstate.isconfirmed and bearInsideSetup[1] and close < low[2] - breakBuffer
    bool bullRangeBreakRaw = usePriceActionBreakouts and barstate.isconfirmed and declineBeforeRange and close > rangeHigh + breakBuffer
    bool bearRangeBreakRaw = usePriceActionBreakouts and barstate.isconfirmed and advanceBeforeRange and close < rangeLow - breakBuffer
    bool bullInsideBreak = bullInsideBreakRaw and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
    bool bearInsideBreak = bearInsideBreakRaw and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
    bool bullRangeBreak = bullRangeBreakRaw and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
    bool bearRangeBreak = bearRangeBreakRaw and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
    [insideBar, bullInsideSetup, bearInsideSetup, volumeAfterDecline, volumeAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, rangeHigh, rangeLow, rangeReady, bullInsideBreakRaw, bearInsideBreakRaw, bullRangeBreakRaw, bearRangeBreakRaw, bullInsideBreak, bearInsideBreak, bullRangeBreak, bearRangeBreak]


[insideBarRaw, bullishInsideBarSetup, bearishInsideBarSetup, volumeRisingAfterDecline, volumeRisingAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, sidewaysRangeHigh, sidewaysRangeLow, sidewaysRangeReady, bullishInsideBarBreakRaw, bearishInsideBarBreakRaw, bullishRangeBreakRaw, bearishRangeBreakRaw, bullishInsideBarBreakout, bearishInsideBarBreakout, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance] = f_price_action_breakouts()
bool bullishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close > high[1] or bullishInsideBarBreakout)
bool bearishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and (bearishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close < low[1] or bearishInsideBarBreakout)
bool bullishCandleImportantContext = bullishWyckoffTest or bullishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bullishDemandRetest or bullishRetest or bullishChoch or bullishBos or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent
bool bearishCandleImportantContext = bearishWyckoffTest or bearishSweep or bearishBreakFailed or bearishSupplyRetest or bearishRetest or bearishChoch or bearishBos or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance or distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent
bool bullishImportantCandleConfirmation = bullishCandleConfirmation and bullishCandleImportantContext
bool bearishImportantCandleConfirmation = bearishCandleConfirmation and bearishCandleImportantContext
int barsSinceBullishCandle = ta.barssince(bullishCandleConfirmation)
int barsSinceBearishCandle = ta.barssince(bearishCandleConfirmation)
bool recentBullishCandle = not na(barsSinceBullishCandle) and barsSinceBullishCandle <= contextLookback
bool recentBearishCandle = not na(barsSinceBearishCandle) and barsSinceBearishCandle <= contextLookback
string confirmedCandleName = f_text_code(TXT_CANDLE_TYPE, candlePatternCode[1])
var string candleState = "لا يوجد نموذج شموعي مؤكد"
if bullishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bullishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار صاعد مؤكد" : confirmedCandleName + " مؤكد"
else if bearishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bearishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار هابط مؤكد" : confirmedCandleName + " مؤكد"

f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state() =>
    var float lastKeyCandleHigh = na
    var float lastKeyCandleLow = na
    var int lastKeyCandleBar = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceHigh = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceLow = na
    var int highVolumeResistanceBar = na
    var float highVolumeSupportHigh = na
    var float highVolumeSupportLow = na
    var int highVolumeSupportBar = na
    bool bullishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    bool bearishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    string currentCandleDescription = "لا وصف"
    string priceActionState = "لا نمط سعري"
    string volumeCandleLevelsState = "لا منطقة حجم"
    int vsaSignal = 0
    string vsaState = "لا إشارة حجم/سلوك"
    // وصف آخر شمعة مغلقة: مختصر، ولا يمثل دخولًا منفردًا.
    bool strongBullCandle = candlePatternCode == 8 or candlePatternCode == 27
    bool strongBearCandle = candlePatternCode == 9 or candlePatternCode == 28
    bool lowerRejectionCandle = bodyRatio <= 0.45 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody * 1.25
    bool upperRejectionCandle = bodyRatio <= 0.45 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody * 1.25
    bullishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and (bullishCandleConfirmation or strongBullCandle) and vwapLongPass
    bearishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and supportClusterActsAsResistance and (bearishCandleConfirmation or strongBearCandle) and vwapShortPass
    int currentCandleBias = bullishReversalPatternRaw or (lowerRejectionCandle and close >= open) or strongBullCandle ? 1 : bearishReversalPatternRaw or (upperRejectionCandle and close <= open) or strongBearCandle ? -1 : 0
    string currentCandleType = f_text_code(TXT_CANDLE_TYPE, candlePatternCode)
    string currentCandleMeaning = bullishInsideBarSetup ? "تجميع بعد هبوط؛ ينتظر كسر الأم" : bearishInsideBarSetup ? "تجميع بعد صعود؛ ينتظر كسر الأم" : insideBarRaw ? "تجميع؛ ينتظر كسر الأم" : bullishReversalPatternRaw ? "انعكاس صاعد؛ ينتظر تأكيد" : bearishReversalPatternRaw ? "انعكاس هابط؛ ينتظر تأكيد" : candlePatternCode == 8 ? "سيطرة شراء" : candlePatternCode == 9 ? "سيطرة بيع" : bodyRatio <= 0.10 or candlePatternCode == 26 ? "تردد" : currentCandleBias == 1 ? "ضغط شراء" : currentCandleBias == -1 ? "ضغط بيع" : "محايد"
    string currentCandleVolumeText = volumeAvailable ? f_ratio(relativeVolume) : "غير متاح"
    currentCandleDescription := currentCandleType + " | " + currentCandleMeaning + " | حجم " + currentCandleVolumeText
    bool highVolumeCandle = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple or heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher)
    bool keyRangeCandleNow = barstate.isconfirmed and (vsaWideResult and (strongBullCandle or strongBearCandle) or highVolumeCandle and (vsaWideResult or vsaNarrowResult))
    if keyRangeCandleNow
        lastKeyCandleHigh := high
        lastKeyCandleLow := low
        lastKeyCandleBar := bar_index
    else if not na(lastKeyCandleBar) and bar_index - lastKeyCandleBar > keyCandleMaximumAge
        lastKeyCandleHigh := na
        lastKeyCandleLow := na
        lastKeyCandleBar := na

    bool highVolumePeakZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingUpContext and (nearStrongResistance or high >= nz(lastSwingHigh, high) - safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation <= 0.55 or upperWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeBottomZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingDownContext and (nearStrongSupport or low <= nz(lastSwingLow, low) + safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation >= 0.45 or lowerWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeResistanceExpired = not na(highVolumeResistanceBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeResistanceBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close > highVolumeResistanceHigh + breakBuffer)
    bool highVolumeSupportExpired = not na(highVolumeSupportBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeSupportBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close < highVolumeSupportLow - breakBuffer)
    if highVolumeResistanceExpired
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := na
        highVolumeResistanceLow := na
        highVolumeResistanceBar := na
    if highVolumeSupportExpired
        highVolumeSupportHigh := na
        highVolumeSupportLow := na
        highVolumeSupportBar := na
    if highVolumePeakZoneEvent
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := high
        highVolumeResistanceLow := low
        highVolumeResistanceBar := bar_index
    if highVolumeBottomZoneEvent
        highVolumeSupportHigh := high
        highVolumeSupportLow := low
        highVolumeSupportBar := bar_index
    bool highVolumeResistanceActive = not na(highVolumeResistanceHigh) and not na(highVolumeResistanceLow)
    bool highVolumeSupportActive = not na(highVolumeSupportHigh) and not na(highVolumeSupportLow)
    priceActionState := bullishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار صاعد مؤكد" : bearishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار هابط مؤكد" : bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline ? "اختراق نطاق بعد هبوط" : bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance ? "كسر نطاق بعد صعود" : bullishInsideBarSetup ? "انسايد بار؛ انتظار اختراق الأم" : bearishInsideBarSetup ? "انسايد بار؛ انتظار كسر الأم" : insideBarRaw ? "انسايد بار؛ انتظار" : sidewaysRangeReady ? "نطاق " + f_price(sidewaysRangeLow) + " - " + f_price(sidewaysRangeHigh) : "لا نمط سعري"
    string volumeTrendText = volumeRisingAfterDecline ? "حجم صاعد بعد هبوط" : volumeRisingAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult ? "حجم صاعد ونتيجة ضعيفة" : "حجم طبيعي"
    string peakVolumeText = highVolumeResistanceActive ? "\nمنطقة حجم مقاومة " + f_price(highVolumeResistanceLow) + " - " + f_price(highVolumeResistanceHigh) : ""
    string bottomVolumeText = highVolumeSupportActive ? "\nمنطقة حجم دعم " + f_price(highVolumeSupportLow) + " - " + f_price(highVolumeSupportHigh) : ""
    volumeCandleLevelsState := volumeTrendText + peakVolumeText + bottomVolumeText
    bool bullishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed)
    bool bearishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed)
    bool bullishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close > high[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or bullishSweep[1] or bearishBreakFailed[1])
    bool bearishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close < low[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or bearishSweep[1] or bullishBreakFailed[1])
    vsaSignal := not useVsaAnalysis ? 0 : (bullishVsaConfirmation or bullishVsaAbsorption or bullishVsaEffortResult) ? 1 : (bearishVsaConfirmation or bearishVsaAbsorption or bearishVsaEffortResult) ? -1 : 0
    int vsaStateCode = (not useVsaAnalysis ? 1 : bullishVsaConfirmation ? 2 : bearishVsaConfirmation ? 3 : not volumeAvailable ? 4 : bullishVsaAbsorption ? 5 : bearishVsaAbsorption ? 6 : bullishVsaEffortResult ? 7 : bearishVsaEffortResult ? 8 : vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult ? 9 : vsaHighEffort ? 10 : 0)
    vsaState := (f_text_code(TXT_VSA_STATE, vsaStateCode))

    [lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, currentCandleDescription, priceActionState, volumeCandleLevelsState, vsaSignal, vsaState]

[lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, currentCandleDescription, priceActionState, volumeCandleLevelsState, vsaSignal, vsaState] = f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state()
// Structural AVWAP starts from a confirmed change, liquidity event, or tested zone.
bool structuralVwapAnchorEvent = (barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishChoch or bearishChoch or bullishSweep or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or bullishDemandRetest or bearishSupplyRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or strongResistanceBreak or strongSupportBreak)) or bullishInsideBarBreakout or bearishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance
float structuralVwapBarVolume = nz(volume, 0.0)
var float structuralVwapPriceVolume = na
var float structuralVwapVolume = na

if not useStructuralVwap
    structuralVwapPriceVolume := na
    structuralVwapVolume := na
else if structuralVwapAnchorEvent
    // A new anchor with no valid volume must invalidate the old structural
    // VWAP. Keeping the previous accumulator would falsely label it as anchored
    // to the newest event.
    structuralVwapPriceVolume := structuralVwapBarVolume > 0 ? hlc3 * structuralVwapBarVolume : na
    structuralVwapVolume := structuralVwapBarVolume > 0 ? structuralVwapBarVolume : na
else if not na(structuralVwapVolume) and structuralVwapVolume > 0 and structuralVwapBarVolume > 0
    structuralVwapPriceVolume += hlc3 * structuralVwapBarVolume
    structuralVwapVolume += structuralVwapBarVolume

bool structuralVwapReady = useStructuralVwap and not na(structuralVwapVolume) and structuralVwapVolume > 0
float structuralVwapValue = structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapPriceVolume / structuralVwapVolume : na
float analysisVwapValue = structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapValue : vwapValue
string analysisVwapLabel = structuralVwapReady ? "هيكلي" : "دوري " + effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe
if barstate.isconfirmed
    confirmedAnalysisVwapValue := analysisVwapValue
    confirmedStructuralVwapReady := structuralVwapReady
// Preserve the exact broken level before the break watcher is reset below.
float confirmedRetestLevel = bullishRetest or bearishRetest ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : na
int generalEventCode = inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed ? 25 : headShouldersConfirmed ? 26 : (doubleBottomConfirmed ? 13 : doubleTopConfirmed ? 14 : bullishChoch ? 19 : bearishChoch ? 20 : bullishBos ? 21 : bearishBos ? 22 : twoSidedLiquiditySweep ? 2 : bullishBreakFailed ? 3 : bearishBreakFailed ? 4 : bullishSweep ? 7 : bearishSweep ? 8 : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? 9 : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? 10 : bullishWyckoffTest ? 5 : bearishWyckoffTest ? 6 : bullishDemandRetest ? 11 : bearishSupplyRetest ? 12 : bullishRetest ? 17 : bearishRetest ? 18 : bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline ? 23 : bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance ? 24 : bullishImportantCandleConfirmation ? 15 : bearishImportantCandleConfirmation ? 16 : wyckoffEventNow ? 1 : 0)
f_general_event_direction(int code) =>
    code == 3 ? -1 : code == 4 ? 1 : code >= 5 and code <= 26 ? (code % 2 == 1 ? 1 : -1) : code == 0 and bullishRsiDivergence ? 1 : code == 0 and bearishRsiDivergence ? -1 : 0

f_general_event_text(int code) =>
    code == 0 ? (bullishRsiDivergence ? "دايفرجنس صاعد" : bearishRsiDivergence ? "دايفرجنس هابط" : "") : code == 15 or code == 16 ? confirmedCandleName : f_text_code(TXT_GENERAL_EVENT, code)

int unifiedEventDirection = f_general_event_direction(generalEventCode)
string unifiedEventText = f_general_event_text(generalEventCode)
int unifiedEventBar = generalEventCode == 13 ? doubleBottomPatternBar : generalEventCode == 14 ? doubleTopPatternBar : generalEventCode == 15 or generalEventCode == 16 ? bar_index - 1 : generalEventCode == 0 and (bullishRsiDivergence or bearishRsiDivergence) ? bar_index - pivotBars : bar_index
float unifiedEventLevel = generalEventCode == 25 ? ihsNecklineNow : generalEventCode == 26 ? hsNecklineNow : generalEventCode == 2 ? close : generalEventCode == 3 or generalEventCode == 4 or generalEventCode == 17 or generalEventCode == 18 ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : generalEventCode == 5 or generalEventCode == 6 ? wyckoffTestLevel : generalEventCode == 7 ? lastSwingLow : generalEventCode == 8 ? lastSwingHigh : generalEventCode == 9 or generalEventCode == 10 ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : generalEventCode == 11 ? demandZoneHigh : generalEventCode == 12 ? supplyZoneLow : generalEventCode == 13 ? doubleBottomPatternLevel : generalEventCode == 14 ? doubleTopPatternLevel : generalEventCode == 19 or generalEventCode == 21 ? lastSwingHigh : generalEventCode == 20 or generalEventCode == 22 ? lastSwingLow : generalEventCode == 15 ? low[1] : generalEventCode == 16 ? high[1] : generalEventCode == 23 ? sidewaysRangeHigh : generalEventCode == 24 ? sidewaysRangeLow : generalEventCode == 0 and bullishRsiDivergence ? pivotLow : generalEventCode == 0 and bearishRsiDivergence ? pivotHigh : na
float unifiedEventAtr = generalEventCode == 15 or generalEventCode == 16 ? nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) : generalEventCode == 0 and (bullishRsiDivergence or bearishRsiDivergence) ? nz(safeAtr[pivotBars], safeAtr) : safeAtr
bool unifiedEventWarning = generalEventCode >= 2 and generalEventCode <= 4 or generalEventCode >= 7 and generalEventCode <= 10
color unifiedEventColor = unifiedEventWarning ? warningColor : unifiedEventDirection == 1 ? bullColor : unifiedEventDirection == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
bool importantGeneralEvent = generalEventCode == 1 or generalEventCode >= 3 and generalEventCode <= 10 or generalEventCode >= 13 and generalEventCode <= 16 or generalEventCode >= 19 and generalEventCode <= 26
bool unifiedEventMarkerEnabled = importantGeneralEvent and unifiedEventText != "" and (generalEventCode == 15 or generalEventCode == 16 ? showImportantCandleLabel : showEventLabel)

float patternFlatSlope = safeAtr * 0.03
string compressionPatternState = not useChartPatterns ? "معطل" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope < -patternFlatSlope and importantLowSlope > patternFlatSlope ? "مثلث متماثل | ضغط" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and math.abs(importantHighSlope) <= patternFlatSlope and importantLowSlope > patternFlatSlope ? "مثلث صاعد | ميل صاعد" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope < -patternFlatSlope and math.abs(importantLowSlope) <= patternFlatSlope ? "مثلث هابط | ميل هابط" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope > patternFlatSlope and importantLowSlope > importantHighSlope ? "وتد صاعد | ضعف محتمل" : importantHighTrendReady and importantLowTrendReady and importantHighSlope < importantLowSlope and importantLowSlope < -patternFlatSlope ? "وتد هابط | تعافٍ محتمل" : convergingImportantStructure ? "تضيق سعري | قرب حركة" : sidewaysRangeReady ? "نطاق عرضي | انتظار كسر" : "لا نموذج"
string technicalPatternState = not useChartPatterns ? "النماذج: معطلة" : inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب | صاعد مؤكد" : headShouldersConfirmed ? "رأس وكتفين | هابط مؤكد" : doubleBottomConfirmed ? "قاعان | صاعد مؤكد" : doubleTopConfirmed ? "قمتان | هابط مؤكد" : inverseHeadShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب | قيد التكوين" : headShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين | قيد التكوين" : doubleBottomArmed ? "قاعان | قيد التكوين" : doubleTopArmed ? "قمتان | قيد التكوين" : compressionPatternState


// Break watcher lifecycle is resolved before re-arming near its definition.
var string lastWyckoffEvent = "لا يوجد"
var string lastClassicEvent = "لا يوجد"
var int lastWyckoffEventBar = na
var int lastClassicEventBar = na

if wyckoffEventNow
    lastWyckoffEvent := wyckoffEventText
    if accumulationSosEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "اختراق مقاومة النطاق"
    else if distributionSowEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "كسر دعم النطاق"
    else if accumulationLpsEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "مقاومة أصبحت دعم"
    else if distributionLpsyEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "دعم أصبح مقاومة"
else if bullishWyckoffTest
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختبار سحب سيولة صاعد"
    lastClassicEvent := "ثبات فوق الدعم"
else if bearishWyckoffTest
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختبار سحب سيولة هابط"
    lastClassicEvent := "ثبات تحت المقاومة"
else if twoSidedLiquiditySweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "سحب سيولة من الجهتين"
    lastClassicEvent := "شمعة خارج الدعم والمقاومة"
else if bullishSweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "سحب سيولة أسفل الدعم"
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر وهمي للدعم"
else if bearishSweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "سحب سيولة أعلى المقاومة"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق وهمي للمقاومة"
else if bearishBreakFailed
    lastWyckoffEvent := "فشل كسر"
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر وهمي"
else if bullishBreakFailed
    lastWyckoffEvent := "فشل اختراق"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق وهمي"
else if inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "رأس وكتفين مقلوب"
else if headShouldersConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "رأس وكتفين"
else if doubleBottomConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "تأكيد قاعين"
else if doubleTopConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "تأكيد قمتين"
else if qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout
    lastClassicEvent := majorChannelLowerFakeout ? "كسر وهمي قناة رئيسية" : "كسر وهمي قناة"
else if qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout
    lastClassicEvent := majorChannelUpperFakeout ? "اختراق وهمي قناة رئيسية" : "اختراق وهمي قناة"
else if bullishDemandRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار طلب"
else if bearishSupplyRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار عرض"
else if bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق نطاق بعد هبوط"
else if bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر نطاق بعد صعود"
else if bullishCandleConfirmation or bearishCandleConfirmation
    lastClassicEvent := candleState
else if bullishRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار صاعدة"
else if bearishRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار هابطة"
else if bullishBreak
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق هيكل مؤكد"
else if bearishBreak
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر هيكل مؤكد"

bool wyckoffDescriptionUpdatedNow = wyckoffEventNow or bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or bullishSweep or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed
bool classicDescriptionUpdatedNow = accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or (generalEventCode != 0 and generalEventCode != 1)
if wyckoffDescriptionUpdatedNow
    lastWyckoffEventBar := bar_index
if classicDescriptionUpdatedNow
    lastClassicEventBar := bar_index
int barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription = na(lastWyckoffEventBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - lastWyckoffEventBar
int barsSinceLastClassicDescription = na(lastClassicEventBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - lastClassicEventBar
string activeWyckoffEventDescription = barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription <= contextLookback ? lastWyckoffEvent + " | " + f_bar_age(barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription) : "لا حدث وايكوف حديث"
string activeClassicEventDescription = barsSinceLastClassicDescription <= contextLookback ? lastClassicEvent + " | " + f_bar_age(barsSinceLastClassicDescription) : "لا حدث فني حديث"

// شروحات الأحداث العامة والشموع تُعرض في اللوحة الثابتة؛ لا توضع نصوص طويلة فوق السعر.

// =============================================================================
// Latest trade plan only
// =============================================================================
// A technical event is snapshotted before all optional participation filters.
// This prevents a valid close beyond structure from disappearing after one bar.
// A plan still requires the original qualifiers or a later confirmed follow-through.
f_contextual_stop_anchor(bool isLong, float fallbackAnchor) =>
    isLong ? (bullishDemandRetest and demandZoneActive ? demandZoneLow : accumulationLpsEvent ? math.min(pivotLow, nz(wyckoffRangeResistance, pivotLow)) : accumulationSpringTestEvent and not na(wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme) ? wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme : bullishWyckoffTest and not na(wyckoffTestExtreme) ? wyckoffTestExtreme : bullishRetest and not na(confirmedRetestLevel) ? math.min(low, confirmedRetestLevel) : nz(lastSwingLow, fallbackAnchor)) : (bearishSupplyRetest and supplyZoneActive ? supplyZoneHigh : distributionLpsyEvent ? math.max(pivotHigh, nz(wyckoffRangeSupport, pivotHigh)) : distributionUtadTestEvent and not na(wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme) ? wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme : bearishWyckoffTest and not na(wyckoffTestExtreme) ? wyckoffTestExtreme : bearishRetest and not na(confirmedRetestLevel) ? math.max(high, confirmedRetestLevel) : nz(lastSwingHigh, fallbackAnchor))

f_plan_setup_source(bool isLong) =>
    isLong ? (bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? "انسايد بار" : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? "اختراق نطاق" : accumulationLpsEvent ? "آخر دعم (LPS)" : bullishDemandRetest ? "منطقة طلب" : accumulationSpringTestEvent ? "اختبار كسر وهمي" : bullishWyckoffTest ? "اختبار سحب سيولة" : bullishRetest ? "إعادة اختبار كسر" : doubleBottomConfirmed ? "قاعان" : accumulationSosEvent ? "قوة (SOS)" : bullishChoch ? "تغير صاعد (CHoCH)" : "كسر صاعد (BOS)") : (bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? "انسايد بار" : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? "كسر نطاق" : distributionLpsyEvent ? "آخر عرض (LPSY)" : bearishSupplyRetest ? "منطقة عرض" : distributionUtadTestEvent ? "اختبار اختراق وهمي" : bearishWyckoffTest ? "اختبار سحب سيولة" : bearishRetest ? "إعادة اختبار كسر" : doubleTopConfirmed ? "قمتان" : distributionSowEvent ? "ضعف (SOW)" : bearishChoch ? "تغير هابط (CHoCH)" : "كسر هابط (BOS)")

type SetupCandidateState
    int dir = 0
    int eventBar = na
    float ref = na
    float rng = na
    float stop = na
    int stopKind = 0
    bool usesGuard = false
    float guardLevel = na
    string source = "-"
    bool directBreak = false
    bool needsQualifiers = false
    bool needsFvg = false
    bool qualified = false
    bool reversalEligible = false

var SetupCandidateState setupCandidateState = SetupCandidateState.new()

f_clear_setup_candidate(SetupCandidateState candidate) =>
    candidate.dir := 0
    candidate.eventBar := na
    candidate.ref := na
    candidate.rng := na
    candidate.stop := na
    candidate.stopKind := 0
    candidate.usesGuard := false
    candidate.guardLevel := na
    candidate.source := "-"
    candidate.directBreak := false
    candidate.needsQualifiers := false
    candidate.needsFvg := false
    candidate.qualified := false
    candidate.reversalEligible := false
    candidate

f_update_setup_candidate_state() =>
    int maximumBars = chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 12 : chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? 8 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 6 : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 5 : chartSeconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 5 : chartSeconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 4 : 3
    int storedCandidateMaximumBars = setupCandidateState.directBreak ? math.max(2, maximumBars - 2) : maximumBars
    bool bullStructure = bullishBreak
    bool bearStructure = bearishBreak
    bool bullChannel = qualifiedChannelBreakUpEvent
    bool bearChannel = qualifiedChannelBreakDownEvent
    bool bullMajor = majorBreakoutUpEvent
    bool bearMajor = majorBreakoutDownEvent
    bool bullHorizontal = strongResistanceBreak
    bool bearHorizontal = strongSupportBreak
    bool bullPattern = inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed
    bool bearPattern = headShouldersConfirmed
    bool bullGenericBreak = bullStructure or bullChannel or bullMajor or bullHorizontal
    bool bearGenericBreak = bearStructure or bearChannel or bearMajor or bearHorizontal
    bool bullBreak = bullGenericBreak or bullPattern or accumulationSosEvent or bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bullishRangeBreakRaw
    bool bearBreak = bearGenericBreak or bearPattern or distributionSowEvent or bearishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw
    bool bullRetestSetup = bullishRetest or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup or bullishDemandRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed
    bool bearRetestSetup = bearishSupplyRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup or bearishRetest or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed
    bool useBreak = entryMode != ENTRY_RETEST
    bool useRetest = entryMode != ENTRY_BREAK
    bool bullEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and ((useBreak and bullBreak) or (useRetest and bullRetestSetup))
    bool bearEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and ((useBreak and bearBreak) or (useRetest and bearRetestSetup))
    int eventDirection = bullEvent == bearEvent ? 0 : bullEvent ? 1 : -1

    bool expired = setupCandidateState.dir != 0 and not na(setupCandidateState.eventBar) and bar_index - setupCandidateState.eventBar > storedCandidateMaximumBars
    bool stopFailed = setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? not na(setupCandidateState.stop) and barstate.isconfirmed and close < setupCandidateState.stop - breakBuffer : setupCandidateState.dir == -1 ? not na(setupCandidateState.stop) and barstate.isconfirmed and close > setupCandidateState.stop + breakBuffer : false
    bool breakFailed = setupCandidateState.directBreak and not na(setupCandidateState.ref) and barstate.isconfirmed and (setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? close < setupCandidateState.ref - retestTolerance * 1.5 : close > setupCandidateState.ref + retestTolerance * 1.5)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and (expired or stopFailed or breakFailed)
        f_clear_setup_candidate(setupCandidateState)

    if barstate.isconfirmed and eventDirection != 0
        bool isLong = eventDirection == 1
        bool directNow = useBreak and (isLong ? bullBreak : bearBreak)
        bool genericBreakNow = isLong ? bullGenericBreak : bearGenericBreak
        bool patternBreakNow = isLong ? bullPattern : bearPattern
        bool needsParticipation = genericBreakNow or patternBreakNow or (isLong ? bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bullishRangeBreakRaw : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw) or directNow and useFvgFilter
        bool needsFvg = directNow and useFvgFilter
        bool qualifiedNow = isLong ? accumulationSosEvent and fvgBullPass or ((bullGenericBreak or bullPattern) and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and fvgBullPass) or bullishInsideBarBreakout and fvgBullPass or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline and fvgBullPass : distributionSowEvent and fvgBearPass or ((bearGenericBreak or bearPattern) and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and fvgBearPass) or bearishInsideBarBreakout and fvgBearPass or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance and fvgBearPass
        bool reversalNow = isLong ? bullishChoch or bullMajor or bullChannel or bullHorizontal or bullPattern or bullishRangeBreakRaw or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent or bullishRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed : bearishChoch or bearMajor or bearChannel or bearHorizontal or bearPattern or bearishRangeBreakRaw or distributionSowEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent or bearishRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed
        float genericSwingRange = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) ? math.abs(lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow) : na
        float reference = isLong ? bullPattern ? ihsNecklineNow : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? high[2] : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bullMajor ? majorChannelUpperNow : bullChannel ? channelUpperNow : bullHorizontal or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? strongResistanceLevel : bullishBreakerRetest ? bullishBreakerHigh : accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent ? wyckoffRangeResistance : bullishDemandRetest ? demandZoneHigh : accumulationSpringTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeSupport : bullishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : bullishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : bearishBreakFailed ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : bearPattern ? hsNecklineNow : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? low[2] : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeLow : bearMajor ? majorChannelLowerNow : bearChannel ? channelLowerNow : bearHorizontal or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? strongSupportLevel : bearishBreakerRetest ? bearishBreakerLow : distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent ? wyckoffRangeSupport : bearishSupplyRetest ? supplyZoneLow : distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeResistance : bearishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : bearishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : bullishBreakFailed ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : nz(lastSwingLow, close)
        float measuredRange = bullPattern ? inverseHeadShouldersHeight : bearPattern ? headShouldersHeight : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? math.abs(high[2] - low[2]) : bullishRangeBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw ? math.abs(sidewaysRangeHigh - sidewaysRangeLow) : bullMajor or bearMajor ? majorChannelWidth : bullChannel or bearChannel ? channelWidth : bullishBreakerRetest ? math.abs(bullishBreakerHigh - bullishBreakerLow) : bearishBreakerRetest ? math.abs(bearishBreakerHigh - bearishBreakerLow) : accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent ? math.abs(nz(wyckoffRangeResistance, close) - nz(wyckoffRangeSupport, close)) : genericSwingRange
        float fallbackDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 1.5, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
        float baseContextStop = f_contextual_stop_anchor(isLong, isLong ? close - fallbackDistance : close + fallbackDistance)
        float contextStop = isLong and bullishBreakerRetest ? bullishBreakerLow : not isLong and bearishBreakerRetest ? bearishBreakerHigh : baseContextStop
        bool protectedStop = isLong ? bullishDemandRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or bullishRetest : bearishSupplyRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or bearishRetest
        float specificStop = isLong ? bullPattern ? ihsRightShoulder : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed ? low : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? low[2] : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeLow : bullMajor ? math.min(low, majorChannelUpperNow) : bullChannel ? math.min(low, channelUpperNow) : bullHorizontal or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? math.min(low, strongResistanceLow) : accumulationSosEvent ? math.min(low, wyckoffRangeResistance) : bullStructure ? math.min(low, lastSwingHigh) : na : bearPattern ? hsRightShoulder : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed ? high : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? high[2] : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bearMajor ? math.max(high, majorChannelLowerNow) : bearChannel ? math.max(high, channelLowerNow) : bearHorizontal or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? math.max(high, strongSupportHigh) : distributionSowEvent ? math.max(high, wyckoffRangeSupport) : bearStructure ? math.max(high, lastSwingLow) : na
        float stopAnchor = protectedStop ? contextStop : not na(specificStop) ? specificStop : contextStop
        int stopKind = protectedStop ? 2 : not na(specificStop) ? 1 : 0
        string suffix = isLong ? bullishBreakerRetest ? " | بريكر صاعد" : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? " | كسر وهمي قناة" : bearishBreakFailed ? " | كسر وهمي" : bullMajor ? " | اختراق قناة رئيسية" : bullChannel ? " | اختراق قناة" : bullHorizontal ? " | اختراق مقاومة" : bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? " | مقاومة أصبحت دعم" : "" : bearishBreakerRetest ? " | بريكر هابط" : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? " | اختراق وهمي قناة" : bullishBreakFailed ? " | اختراق وهمي" : bearMajor ? " | كسر قناة رئيسية" : bearChannel ? " | كسر قناة" : bearHorizontal ? " | كسر دعم" : bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? " | دعم أصبح مقاومة" : ""
        string source = bullPattern ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب" : bearPattern ? "رأس وكتفين" : f_plan_setup_source(isLong) + suffix
        bool preserveQualified = setupCandidateState.dir != 0 and setupCandidateState.qualified and (setupCandidateState.dir == eventDirection or setupCandidateState.dir != eventDirection and needsParticipation and not qualifiedNow)
        if not preserveQualified or not needsParticipation or qualifiedNow
            bool priorQualified = setupCandidateState.dir == eventDirection and setupCandidateState.qualified
            bool priorReversal = setupCandidateState.dir == eventDirection and setupCandidateState.reversalEligible
            bool usesGuard = isLong ? bullStructure or bullishRetest : bearStructure or bearishRetest
            float guardLevel = isLong ? bullStructure ? lastSwingHigh : bullishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : na : bearStructure ? lastSwingLow : bearishRetest ? confirmedRetestLevel : na
            setupCandidateState.dir := eventDirection
            setupCandidateState.eventBar := bar_index
            setupCandidateState.ref := reference
            setupCandidateState.rng := measuredRange
            setupCandidateState.stop := stopAnchor
            setupCandidateState.stopKind := stopKind
            setupCandidateState.usesGuard := usesGuard
            setupCandidateState.guardLevel := guardLevel
            setupCandidateState.source := source
            setupCandidateState.directBreak := directNow
            setupCandidateState.needsQualifiers := needsParticipation
            setupCandidateState.needsFvg := needsFvg
            setupCandidateState.qualified := priorQualified or qualifiedNow
            setupCandidateState.reversalEligible := priorReversal or reversalNow
    else if barstate.isconfirmed and bullEvent and bearEvent and setupCandidateState.dir == 0
        setupCandidateState.source := "تعارض شراء وبيع"

    int activeMaximumBars = setupCandidateState.directBreak ? math.max(2, maximumBars - 2) : maximumBars
    bool withinWindow = setupCandidateState.dir != 0 and not na(setupCandidateState.eventBar) and bar_index - setupCandidateState.eventBar <= activeMaximumBars
    bool stopHolds = setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? na(setupCandidateState.stop) or close >= setupCandidateState.stop - breakBuffer : setupCandidateState.dir == -1 ? na(setupCandidateState.stop) or close <= setupCandidateState.stop + breakBuffer : false
    bool breakHolds = not setupCandidateState.directBreak or na(setupCandidateState.ref) or (setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? close >= setupCandidateState.ref - retestTolerance * 1.5 : close <= setupCandidateState.ref + retestTolerance * 1.5)
    bool qualityPass = setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? bullishBreakCandleQuality : setupCandidateState.dir == -1 ? bearishBreakCandleQuality : false
    bool volumeFilterPass = not setupCandidateState.needsQualifiers or volumePass
    bool fvgFilterPass = not setupCandidateState.needsFvg or (setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? fvgBullPass : setupCandidateState.dir == -1 ? fvgBearPass : false)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and breakHolds and qualityPass and volumeFilterPass and fvgFilterPass
        setupCandidateState.qualified := true
    bool entryQualified = not setupCandidateState.needsQualifiers or setupCandidateState.qualified
    bool active = withinWindow and stopHolds and breakHolds
    float entryReferenceRange = math.max(nz(setupCandidateState.rng, safeAtr * 2.0), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float directMaximumExtension = useAdaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(safeAtr * 1.50, math.max(safeAtr * 0.75, entryReferenceRange * 0.30)) : math.min(safeAtr * 2.0, math.max(safeAtr, entryReferenceRange * 0.35))
    float retestMaximumDistance = useAdaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(safeAtr, math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, entryReferenceRange * 0.18)) : math.min(safeAtr * 1.25, math.max(safeAtr * 0.50, entryReferenceRange * 0.20))
    bool minimumBreakLevelPass = not setupCandidateState.directBreak or na(setupCandidateState.ref) or (setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? close >= setupCandidateState.ref - retestTolerance : close <= setupCandidateState.ref + retestTolerance)
    bool extensionPass = not preventExtendedEntries or na(setupCandidateState.ref) or (setupCandidateState.directBreak ? (setupCandidateState.dir == 1 ? close <= setupCandidateState.ref + directMaximumExtension : close >= setupCandidateState.ref - directMaximumExtension) : math.abs(close - setupCandidateState.ref) <= retestMaximumDistance)
    bool entryLevelPass = minimumBreakLevelPass and extensionPass
    bool longActionable = stockChartAllowed and entrySessionPass and active and entryQualified and entryLevelPass and setupCandidateState.dir == 1 and vwapLongPass
    bool shortActionable = stockChartAllowed and entrySessionPass and active and entryQualified and entryLevelPass and setupCandidateState.dir == -1 and vwapShortPass
    [activeMaximumBars, active, longActionable, shortActionable]

[setupCandidateMaximumBars, setupCandidateActive, actionableLongSetup, actionableShortSetup] = f_update_setup_candidate_state()
int setupCandidateDirection = setupCandidateState.dir
float setupCandidateReference = setupCandidateState.ref
// ICT premium/discount is tied to the ordered, confirmed swing leg. It is a
// location preference for retests only, never an independent breakout trigger.
f_dealing_range_context() =>
    bool rangeReady = usePremiumDiscountContext and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastSwingHighBar) and not na(lastSwingLowBar) and lastSwingHigh > lastSwingLow
    bool bullishLeg = rangeReady and lastSwingLowBar < lastSwingHighBar
    bool bearishLeg = rangeReady and lastSwingHighBar < lastSwingLowBar
    float rangeSize = rangeReady ? lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow : na
    float midpoint = rangeReady ? (lastSwingHigh + lastSwingLow) * 0.50 : na
    bool insideRange = rangeReady and close >= lastSwingLow and close <= lastSwingHigh
    float bullishOteLower = bullishLeg ? lastSwingHigh - rangeSize * 0.79 : na
    float bullishOteUpper = bullishLeg ? lastSwingHigh - rangeSize * 0.62 : na
    float bearishOteLower = bearishLeg ? lastSwingLow + rangeSize * 0.62 : na
    float bearishOteUpper = bearishLeg ? lastSwingLow + rangeSize * 0.79 : na
    bool bullishOte = bullishLeg and insideRange and close >= bullishOteLower and close <= bullishOteUpper
    bool bearishOte = bearishLeg and insideRange and close >= bearishOteLower and close <= bearishOteUpper
    bool evidenceReady = (setupCandidateActive and not setupCandidateState.directBreak) and insideRange and (bullishLeg or bearishLeg)
    int signal = not evidenceReady ? 0 : bullishLeg and close <= midpoint ? 1 : bearishLeg and close >= midpoint ? -1 : 0
    string stateText = not usePremiumDiscountContext ? "العلاوة/الخصم: معطل" : not rangeReady ? "العلاوة/الخصم: انتظار" : not insideRange ? "خارج آخر نطاق" : bullishLeg ? (bullishOte ? "داخل OTE شراء 62-79%" : close <= midpoint ? "خصم؛ خارج OTE" : "علاوة") : bearishLeg ? (bearishOte ? "داخل OTE بيع 62-79%" : close >= midpoint ? "علاوة؛ خارج OTE" : "خصم") : "نطاق"
    [evidenceReady, signal, stateText]


[dealingRangeEvidenceReady, dealingRangeSignal, dealingRangeState] = f_dealing_range_context()
f_fib_retracement_context() =>
    bool retracementReady = useFibRetracement and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastSwingHighBar) and not na(lastSwingLowBar) and lastSwingHigh > lastSwingLow and lastSwingHighBar != lastSwingLowBar
    bool bullishLeg = retracementReady and lastSwingLowBar < lastSwingHighBar
    bool bearishLeg = retracementReady and lastSwingHighBar < lastSwingLowBar
    float legRange = retracementReady ? lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow : na
    float level236 = bullishLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.236 : bearishLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.236 : na
    float level382 = bullishLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.382 : bearishLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.382 : na
    float level500 = bullishLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.500 : bearishLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.500 : na
    float level618 = bullishLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.618 : bearishLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.618 : na
    float level786 = bullishLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.786 : bearishLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.786 : na
    float level1000 = bullishLeg ? lastSwingLow : bearishLeg ? lastSwingHigh : na
    string depthText = not retracementReady ? "" : bullishLeg ? close >= level236 ? "23.6%" : close >= level382 ? "23.6%|38.2%" : close >= level500 ? "38.2%|50%" : close >= level618 ? "50%|61.8%" : close >= level786 ? "61.8%|78.6%" : close >= level1000 ? "78.6%|100%" : ">100%" : close <= level236 ? "23.6%" : close <= level382 ? "23.6%|38.2%" : close <= level500 ? "38.2%|50%" : close <= level618 ? "50%|61.8%" : close <= level786 ? "61.8%|78.6%" : close <= level1000 ? "78.6%|100%" : ">100%"
    string directionText = bullishLeg ? "صعود" : bearishLeg ? "هبوط" : ""
    string levelsText = retracementReady ? "23.6 " + f_price(level236) + " | 38.2 " + f_price(level382) + " | 50 " + f_price(level500) + "\n61.8 " + f_price(level618) + " | 78.6 " + f_price(level786) + " | 100 " + f_price(level1000) : ""
    string stateText = not useFibRetracement ? "فيبوناتشي: معطل" : not retracementReady ? "فيبوناتشي: انتظار" : directionText + " | عمق " + depthText + "\n" + levelsText
    [retracementReady, bullishLeg, bearishLeg, level236, level382, level500, level618, level786, level1000, stateText]


[fibRetracementReady, fibBullishLeg, fibBearishLeg, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, fibRetracementState] = f_fib_retracement_context()
float fibStopMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
float fibTargetMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
float nearestFibSupportForStop = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibResistanceForStop = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibSupportForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibResistanceForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)

// =============================================================================
// التوافق مقسم إلى مجموعات مستقلة قدر الإمكان، ويستخدم مقاما ديناميكيا حسب
// الأدوات المتاحة والمفعلة بدلا من عد الحدث السعري نفسه عدة مرات.
f_calculate_evidence_aggregates() =>
    int evidenceMaximum = 1
    int bullishEvidence = 0
    int bearishEvidence = 0
    float bullishEvidencePercent = 0.0
    float bearishEvidencePercent = 0.0
    bool swingStructureReady = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and swingLevelsCompatible
    bool higherStructureReady = not na(higherClose) and not na(higherEma20) and not na(higherEma50)
    int structureEvidenceMax = (swingStructureReady ? 1 : 0) + (higherStructureReady ? 1 : 0)
    int bullishStructureEvidence = (swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (higherStructureReady and higherTrendBias == 1 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishStructureEvidence = (swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (higherStructureReady and higherTrendBias == -1 ? 1 : 0)

    // Direct RVOL and VSA are correlated because VSA already contains volume. Keep
    // them in one participation group so the same candle cannot earn two votes.
    bool directVolumeEvidenceEnabled = useVolumeFilter and setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateState.directBreak and volumeAvailable
    bool vsaEvidenceEnabled = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable
    int participationEvidenceMax = directVolumeEvidenceEnabled or vsaEvidenceEnabled ? 1 : 0
    int bullishParticipationEvidence = (directVolumeEvidenceEnabled and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation >= 0.55) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == 1) ? 1 : 0
    int bearishParticipationEvidence = (directVolumeEvidenceEnabled and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation <= 0.45) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == -1) ? 1 : 0

    // If the hard VWAP requirement is enabled, score against the very same entry
    // reference. With the hard filter disabled, only an explicitly enabled and ready
    // structural VWAP contributes soft evidence; periodic VWAP does not sneak back in.
    float evidenceVwapValue = useVwapFilter ? entryVwapValue : useStructuralVwap and structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapValue : na
    bool vwapEvidenceReady = not na(evidenceVwapValue)
    bool zoneEvidenceReady = demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant
    bool horizontalSrEvidenceReady = horizontalSrSignal != 0
    int rawLocationEvidenceMax = (channelEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0)
    int locationEvidenceMax = math.min(3, rawLocationEvidenceMax)
    int bullishLocationRaw = (channelEvidenceReady and effectiveChannelSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close > evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishLocationRaw = (channelEvidenceReady and effectiveChannelSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close < evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0)
    int bullishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, bullishLocationRaw)
    int bearishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, bearishLocationRaw)

    int barsSinceBullishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bullishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline)
    int barsSinceBearishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bearishInsideBarBreakout or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance)
    bool recentBullishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBullishPriceAction) and barsSinceBullishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    bool recentBearishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBearishPriceAction) and barsSinceBearishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    int triggerEvidenceMax = math.min(2, 1 + (useCandleConfirmation ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence ? 1 : 0))
    int bullishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (recentBullishAuctionTrigger ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBullishCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBullishPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBullishDivergence ? 1 : 0))
    int bearishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (recentBearishAuctionTrigger ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBearishCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBearishPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBearishDivergence ? 1 : 0))

    evidenceMaximum := math.max(1, structureEvidenceMax + participationEvidenceMax + locationEvidenceMax + triggerEvidenceMax)
    bullishEvidence := bullishStructureEvidence + bullishParticipationEvidence + bullishLocationEvidence + bullishTriggerEvidence
    bearishEvidence := bearishStructureEvidence + bearishParticipationEvidence + bearishLocationEvidence + bearishTriggerEvidence
    bullishEvidencePercent := 100.0 * float(bullishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum)
    bearishEvidencePercent := 100.0 * float(bearishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum)


    [evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent]

[evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent] = f_calculate_evidence_aggregates()
// Preliminary opportunity layer. It may update while the realtime candle forms,
// but it never creates an entry, stop, targets, or trade alert. Confirmed setup
// candidates above are close-based and persist independently.
f_calculate_formation_watch() =>
    int formationDirection = 0
    string formationSource = "لا مراقبة"
    float formationEvidenceMinimum = math.max(25.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - 30.0)
    float formationProximity = safeAtr * (chartSeconds <= 15 * 60 ? 0.35 : chartSeconds <= 60 * 60 ? 0.45 : 0.60)
    float bullishWatchLevel = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, syminfo.mintick, not highWasBroken ? lastSwingHigh : na, strongResistanceReady and not strongResistanceRoleReversed ? strongResistanceHigh : na, channelReady ? channelUpperNow : na, majorChannelReady ? majorChannelUpperNow : na, bullishInsideBarSetup ? high[1] : na, sidewaysRangeReady and precedingDownContext ? sidewaysRangeHigh : na, wyckoffMode == 1 ? wyckoffRangeResistance : na, na, na, na)
    float bearishWatchLevel = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, syminfo.mintick, not lowWasBroken ? lastSwingLow : na, strongSupportReady and not strongSupportRoleReversed ? strongSupportLow : na, channelReady ? channelLowerNow : na, majorChannelReady ? majorChannelLowerNow : na, bearishInsideBarSetup ? low[1] : na, sidewaysRangeReady and precedingUpContext ? sidewaysRangeLow : na, wyckoffMode == -1 ? wyckoffRangeSupport : na, na, na, na)
    bool bullishApproachWatch = not na(bullishWatchLevel) and bullishWatchLevel - close <= formationProximity and high >= bullishWatchLevel - formationProximity
    bool bearishApproachWatch = not na(bearishWatchLevel) and close - bearishWatchLevel <= formationProximity and low <= bearishWatchLevel + formationProximity
    bool bullishRetestWatch = (watchAfterBreak and priorWatchedBreakDirection == 1 and low <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance * 1.5 and close >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance) or (demandZoneActive and low <= demandZoneHigh + retestTolerance and close >= demandZoneLow) or (bullishBreakerActive and low <= bullishBreakerHigh + retestTolerance and close >= bullishBreakerLow) or resistanceClusterActsAsSupport
    bool bearishRetestWatch = (watchAfterBreak and priorWatchedBreakDirection == -1 and high >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance * 1.5 and close <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance) or (supplyZoneActive and high >= supplyZoneLow - retestTolerance and close <= supplyZoneHigh) or (bearishBreakerActive and high >= bearishBreakerLow - retestTolerance and close <= bearishBreakerHigh) or supportClusterActsAsResistance
    bool bullishFormationContext = bullishInsideBarSetup or (sidewaysRangeReady and precedingDownContext) or useCandleConfirmation and bullishReversalPatternRaw or inverseHeadShouldersReady or accumulationSpringEvent or bullishSweep
    bool bearishFormationContext = bearishInsideBarSetup or (sidewaysRangeReady and precedingUpContext) or useCandleConfirmation and bearishReversalPatternRaw or headShouldersReady or distributionUtadEvent or bearishSweep
    bool bullishFormationRaw = stockChartAllowed and (bullishApproachWatch or bullishRetestWatch or bullishFormationContext) and bullishEvidencePercent >= formationEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence
    bool bearishFormationRaw = stockChartAllowed and (bearishApproachWatch or bearishRetestWatch or bearishFormationContext) and bearishEvidencePercent >= formationEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence
    formationDirection := bullishFormationRaw and not bearishFormationRaw ? 1 : bearishFormationRaw and not bullishFormationRaw ? -1 : 0
    formationSource := formationDirection == 1 ? (bullishRetestWatch ? "إعادة اختبار أو طلب" : inverseHeadShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب" : bullishInsideBarSetup ? "انسايد بار" : sidewaysRangeReady and precedingDownContext ? "نطاق بعد هبوط" : useCandleConfirmation and bullishReversalPatternRaw ? "شمعة انعكاس صاعد" : accumulationSpringEvent or bullishSweep ? "سحب سيولة" : "قرب اختراق") : formationDirection == -1 ? (bearishRetestWatch ? "إعادة اختبار أو عرض" : headShouldersReady ? "رأس وكتفين" : bearishInsideBarSetup ? "انسايد بار" : sidewaysRangeReady and precedingUpContext ? "نطاق بعد صعود" : useCandleConfirmation and bearishReversalPatternRaw ? "شمعة انعكاس هابط" : distributionUtadEvent or bearishSweep ? "سحب سيولة" : "قرب كسر") : "لا مراقبة"

    [formationDirection, formationSource]

[formationDirection, formationSource] = f_calculate_formation_watch()
int combinedTechnicalOpinionCode = (not swingLevelsCompatible ? 1 : structureDirectionConflict ? 2 : channelLayerConflict ? 9 : bullishEvidencePercent >= 70 and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2 ? 3 : bearishEvidencePercent >= 70 and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2 ? 4 : resolvedStructureBias == 1 and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence ? 5 : resolvedStructureBias == -1 and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence ? 6 : bullishEvidencePercent >= 55 and bullishEvidence > bearishEvidence ? 7 : bearishEvidencePercent >= 55 and bearishEvidence > bullishEvidence ? 8 : 0)
string combinedTechnicalOpinion = (combinedTechnicalOpinionCode == 0 ? technicalOpinion : f_text_code(TXT_COMBINED_OPINION, combinedTechnicalOpinionCode))
bool bullishCandleIsLatest = recentBullishCandle and (not recentBearishCandle or barsSinceBullishCandle <= barsSinceBearishCandle)
bool bullishDivergenceIsLatest = recentBullishDivergence and (not recentBearishDivergence or barsSinceBullishDivergence <= barsSinceBearishDivergence)
string activeCandleState = bullishCandleIsLatest ? "شمعة صاعدة " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBullishCandle) : recentBearishCandle ? "شمعة هابطة " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBearishCandle) : "لا شمعة مؤكدة حديثة"
string activeDivergenceState = bullishDivergenceIsLatest ? "دايفرجنس RSI صاعد " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBullishDivergence) : recentBearishDivergence ? "دايفرجنس RSI هابط " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBearishDivergence) : "لا دايفرجنس حديث"
string behaviorState = currentCandleDescription + "\n" + activeCandleState + " | " + activeDivergenceState + "\n" + priceActionState
string higherContextState = higherTrendState + " | صعود " + str.tostring(bullishEvidence) + "/" + str.tostring(evidenceMaximum) + " | هبوط " + str.tostring(bearishEvidence) + "/" + str.tostring(evidenceMaximum)

// The dashboard always displays the last confirmed close, including while a new candle is forming.
var int closedStructureBias = 0
var bool closedStructureConflict = false
var int closedDowBias = 0
var string closedDowState = "داو: انتظار"
var string closedWyckoffEvent = "لا يوجد"
var string closedWyckoffPhaseState = "لا تسلسل وايكوف"
var string closedWyckoffTrendContextState = "لا سياق"
var string closedWyckoffRangeState = "نطاق وايكوف: انتظار"
var int closedVsaSignal = 0
var string closedVsaState = "لا إشارة حجم/سلوك"
var string closedVolumeCandleLevelsState = "لا منطقة حجم"
var string closedClassicEvent = "لا يوجد"
var int closedZoneSignal = 0
var string closedZoneState = "لا منطقة"
var int closedHorizontalSrSignal = 0
var string closedHorizontalSrState = "لا مستوى قريب"
var string closedBehaviorState = "لا تأكيد سلوكي"
var string closedTechnicalPatternState = "لا نموذج فني"
var int closedHigherTrendBias = 0
var string closedHigherContextState = "الإطار الأعلى: انتظار"
var int closedMajorChannelSignal = 0
var string closedMajorChannelState = "قناة رئيسية: انتظار"
var int closedChannelSignal = 0
var string closedChannelState = "قناة: انتظار"
var string closedTechnicalOpinion = "محايد"
var bool closedVolumeAvailable = false
var float closedRelativeVolume = na
var bool closedRecentBullFvg = false
var bool closedRecentBearFvg = false
var string closedVwapLabel = "دوري"
var string closedDealingRangeState = "العلاوة/الخصم: انتظار"
var string closedFibRetracementState = "فيبوناتشي: انتظار"
var string closedMomentumContextState = "زخم: انتظار"

if barstate.isconfirmed
    closedStructureBias := resolvedStructureBias
    closedStructureConflict := structureDirectionConflict
    closedDowBias := dowBias
    closedDowState := dowState
    closedWyckoffEvent := wyckoffMode == 0 ? "لا حدث وايكوف حديث" : activeWyckoffEventDescription
    closedWyckoffPhaseState := wyckoffPhaseState
    closedWyckoffTrendContextState := wyckoffTrendContextState
    closedWyckoffRangeState := wyckoffRangeState
    closedVsaSignal := vsaSignal
    closedVsaState := vsaState
    closedVolumeCandleLevelsState := volumeCandleLevelsState
    closedClassicEvent := activeClassicEventDescription
    closedZoneSignal := zoneSignal
    closedZoneState := zoneState
    closedHorizontalSrSignal := horizontalSrSignal
    closedHorizontalSrState := horizontalSrState
    closedBehaviorState := behaviorState
    closedTechnicalPatternState := technicalPatternState
    closedHigherTrendBias := higherTrendBias
    closedHigherContextState := higherContextState
    closedMajorChannelSignal := majorChannelSignal
    closedMajorChannelState := majorChannelState
    closedChannelSignal := channelSignal
    closedChannelState := channelState
    closedTechnicalOpinion := combinedTechnicalOpinion
    closedVolumeAvailable := volumeAvailable
    closedRelativeVolume := relativeVolume
    closedRecentBullFvg := recentBullFvg
    closedRecentBearFvg := recentBearFvg
    closedVwapLabel := analysisVwapLabel
    closedDealingRangeState := dealingRangeState
    closedFibRetracementState := fibRetracementState
    closedMomentumContextState := momentumContextState

// varip is intentional only for the live plan state: it prevents the same
// intrabar touch from being lost or counted twice on live updates. Entries and all
// analytical setups remain close-confirmed. Historical OHLC cannot reconstruct
// the exact order of a stop and target touched within the same candle.
varip int planDirection = 0
varip bool planActive = false
varip int planStartBar = na
varip float planEntry = na
varip float planStop = na
varip float planTarget1 = na
varip float planTarget2 = na
varip float planTarget3 = na
varip float planInvalidationLevel = na
varip int planScore = 0
varip int planScoreMax = 1
varip bool target1Done = false
varip bool target2Done = false
varip bool target3Done = false
varip bool planTrailingStopActive = false
varip int planTrailingStopStartedBar = na
varip int target1HitBar = na
varip int target2HitBar = na
varip int target3HitBar = na
varip float planHighSinceEntry = na
varip float planLowSinceEntry = na
varip bool planCounterTrend = false
varip string planState = "لا خطة"
varip string planTradeText = "لا صفقة"
varip string planPriceContext = "-"
varip string planSetupSource = "-"
varip string planTargetHorizon = "-"
varip bool planUsesStructureBreakGuard = false
varip float planStructureBreakGuardLevel = na
varip string lastPlanResult = "لا نتيجة"
varip int lastPlanExitBar = na
varip int planOutcomeBar = na
varip int planOutcomeKind = 0
varip float planOutcomePrice = na

var line entryLine = na
var line stopLine = na
var line target1Line = na
var line target2Line = na
var line target3Line = na
var label planBox = na
var label entryLevelLabel = na
var label stopLevelLabel = na
var label target1LevelLabel = na
var label target2LevelLabel = na
var label target3LevelLabel = na
var label activePlanEntryMarker = na
var label activePlanTarget1Marker = na
var label activePlanTarget2Marker = na
var label activePlanTarget3Marker = na
// قيّم الخطة القائمة أولا. بهذه الأولوية لا تستطيع إشارة جديدة أن تمسح وقفا
// أو هدفا حدث على الشمعة نفسها. لمس الوقف له الأولوية المحافظة، ثم الإبطال، ثم الأهداف.
if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    planHighSinceEntry := na(planHighSinceEntry) ? high : math.max(planHighSinceEntry, high)
    planLowSinceEntry := na(planLowSinceEntry) ? low : math.min(planLowSinceEntry, low)

bool planStopTouchedNow = planActive and bar_index > planStartBar and (planDirection == 1 ? low <= planStop : high >= planStop)
bool planInvalidatedByClose = planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and not na(planInvalidationLevel) and ((planDirection == 1 and close < planInvalidationLevel) or (planDirection == -1 and close > planInvalidationLevel))
bool planBreakFailedNow = planActive and planUsesStructureBreakGuard and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and not na(resolvedBreakEventLevel) and not na(planStructureBreakGuardLevel) and math.abs(resolvedBreakEventLevel - planStructureBreakGuardLevel) <= math.max(retestTolerance, syminfo.mintick * 2.0) and ((planDirection == 1 and bullishBreakFailed) or (planDirection == -1 and bearishBreakFailed))

if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    bool target1Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget1 : low <= planTarget1
    bool target2Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget2 : low <= planTarget2
    bool target3Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget3 : low <= planTarget3

    if planStopTouchedNow
        bool openedBeyondStop = planDirection == 1 ? open < planStop : open > planStop
        // On realtime bars varip tells us whether T1/T2 was already recorded on
        // an earlier tick. Preserve that known order. Only a still-unrecorded
        // target appearing together with the stop is ambiguous and loses to the
        // stop. Historical OHLC naturally takes this conservative branch.
        bool targetCompletedEarlierThisBar = (target1Done and target1HitBar == bar_index) or (target2Done and target2HitBar == bar_index)
        bool unorderedTargetTouchedNow = (not target1Done and target1Touched) or (not target2Done and target2Touched) or (not target3Done and target3Touched)
        bool targetTouchedOnStopBar = targetCompletedEarlierThisBar or unorderedTargetTouchedNow
        float executableStopPrice = openedBeyondStop ? open : planStop
        planActive := false
        string stopKindText = planTrailingStopActive ? "وقف متحرك" : "وقف"
        string stopBaseState = target2Done ? stopKindText + " بعد هدف 2" : target1Done ? stopKindText + " بعد هدف 1" : stopKindText
        string stopGapState = openedBeyondStop ? " | تنفيذ فجوة" : ""
        string stopSequenceState = unorderedTargetTouchedNow ? " | أولوية الوقف" : targetCompletedEarlierThisBar ? " | هدف قبل الوقف" : ""
        planState := stopBaseState + stopGapState + stopSequenceState
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 1
        planOutcomePrice := executableStopPrice
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 12, 2)
        if not target1Done
            f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        if not target2Done
            f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        if not target3Done
            f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        planBox := f_clear_label(planBox)
    else if planInvalidatedByClose or planBreakFailedNow
        planActive := false
        planState := planInvalidatedByClose ? "إلغاء: تجاوز الإبطال" : "إلغاء: كسر وهمي"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 2
        planOutcomePrice := close
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 78, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        planBox := f_clear_label(planBox)
    else if target3Touched and not target3Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        if not target2Done
            target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        target3Done := true
        target3HitBar := bar_index
        planActive := false
        planState := "تحقق هدف 3"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 3
        planOutcomePrice := planTarget3
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
        target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
        target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
        planBox := f_clear_label(planBox)
    else if target2Touched and not target2Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        planState := "تحقق هدف 2"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
        target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
    else if target1Touched and not target1Done
        target1Done := true
        target1HitBar := bar_index
        planState := "تحقق هدف 1"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)

// The stop used to evaluate this bar was fixed on the preceding execution.
// Only after the confirmed close do we calculate the next bar's protected
// stop. This prevents the current high from raising a stop that is then tested
// against the current low when their true intrabar order is unknown.
if planActive and usePostTargetAtrTrail and target1Done and barstate.isconfirmed and postTargetTrailReady
    float rawTrailCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? planHighSinceEntry - postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple : planLowSinceEntry + postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple
    float breakEvenProtectedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.max(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry) : math.min(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry)
    float priceValidCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.min(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close + syminfo.mintick)
    bool candidateProtectsEntry = planDirection == 1 ? priceValidCandidate >= planEntry : priceValidCandidate <= planEntry
    bool candidateImprovesStop = planDirection == 1 ? priceValidCandidate > planStop + syminfo.mintick : priceValidCandidate < planStop - syminfo.mintick
    if candidateProtectsEntry and candidateImprovesStop
        planStop := priceValidCandidate
        planTrailingStopActive := true
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na(planTrailingStopStartedBar) ? bar_index : planTrailingStopStartedBar
        planState := target2Done ? "هدف 2 | وقف متحرك" : "هدف 1 | وقف متحرك"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        if not na(stopLine)
            line.set_xy1(stopLine, bar_index, planStop)
            line.set_xy2(stopLine, bar_index + 1, planStop)
            line.set_extend(stopLine, extend.right)
            line.set_color(stopLine, color.new(#F23645, planStopLineTransparency))
            line.set_style(stopLine, line.style_dotted)
            line.set_width(stopLine, 1)

int setupCandidateAge = setupCandidateActive and not na(setupCandidateState.eventBar) ? bar_index - setupCandidateState.eventBar : 100000
int earlyRetestMaximumAge = chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 2 : 1
bool freshRetestCandidate = allowEarlyQualifiedRetest and setupCandidateActive and not setupCandidateState.directBreak and setupCandidateState.reversalEligible and setupCandidateAge <= earlyRetestMaximumAge
bool bullishEarlyTriggerNow = freshRetestCandidate and setupCandidateDirection == 1 and (bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or bullishDemandRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup or bullishRetest or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed)
bool bearishEarlyTriggerNow = freshRetestCandidate and setupCandidateDirection == -1 and (bearishWyckoffTest or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or bearishSupplyRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup or bearishRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed)
bool bullishEarlyCloseQuality = bullishEarlyTriggerNow and close > open and closeLocation >= 0.55 and vsaSignal != -1
bool bearishEarlyCloseQuality = bearishEarlyTriggerNow and close < open and closeLocation <= 0.45 and vsaSignal != 1
float entryClusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 8.0)
float entryClusterReference = setupCandidateActive ? setupCandidateReference : na
float entryStructureSwing = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? lastSwingHigh : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? lastSwingLow : na
float entryHorizontalLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? strongResistanceLevel : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? strongSupportLevel : na
float entryZoneLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? (bullishBreakerActive and not na(entryClusterReference) and math.abs(entryClusterReference - bullishBreakerHigh) <= entryClusterTolerance ? bullishBreakerHigh : supplyZoneLow) : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? (bearishBreakerActive and not na(entryClusterReference) and math.abs(entryClusterReference - bearishBreakerLow) <= entryClusterTolerance ? bearishBreakerLow : demandZoneHigh) : na
float entryWyckoffLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? wyckoffRangeResistance : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? wyckoffRangeSupport : na
float entryMinorChannelLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? channelUpperNow : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? channelLowerNow : na
float entryMajorChannelLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? majorChannelUpperNow : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? majorChannelLowerNow : na
float entryFibLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? nearestFibResistanceForTarget : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? nearestFibSupportForTarget : na
float entryVolumeCandleLevel = setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? highVolumeResistanceLow : setupCandidateDirection == -1 ? highVolumeSupportHigh : na
int entryClusterCount = f_cluster_count(entryClusterReference, entryClusterTolerance, entryStructureSwing, entryHorizontalLevel, entryZoneLevel, entryWyckoffLevel, entryMinorChannelLevel, entryMajorChannelLevel, entryFibLevel, ema20, ema50, entryVolumeCandleLevel)
bool strongEntryCluster = entryClusterCount >= 2
float clusterEvidenceDiscount = strongEntryCluster ? 2.5 : 0.0
float effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum = math.max(50.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - clusterEvidenceDiscount)
float directEvidenceMinimum = math.max(minimumPlanEvidencePercent, math.min(90.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 5.0 - clusterEvidenceDiscount))
float earlyRetestEvidenceMinimum = math.max(50.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - 5.0 - clusterEvidenceDiscount)
bool longEvidencePass = not useAdaptiveEntryQuality ? bullishEvidencePercent >= effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence > bearishEvidence : setupCandidateState.directBreak ? bullishEvidencePercent >= directEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2 : bullishEarlyCloseQuality ? bullishEvidencePercent >= earlyRetestEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 1 : bullishEvidencePercent >= effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence > bearishEvidence
bool shortEvidencePass = not useAdaptiveEntryQuality ? bearishEvidencePercent >= effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence > bullishEvidence : setupCandidateState.directBreak ? bearishEvidencePercent >= directEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2 : bearishEarlyCloseQuality ? bearishEvidencePercent >= earlyRetestEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 1 : bearishEvidencePercent >= effectiveNormalEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence > bullishEvidence
// الاتجاهات ليست أصواتا مكررة فقط؛ هي بوابة سلامة. يمنع التعارض البنيوي
