// أو تعارض القناتين الخطة العادية. الاستثناء الوحيد هو انعكاس مؤكد غيّر
// الهيكل وامتلك فارق أدلة واضحا مع كسر/اختبار فني معتبر.
float counterTrendEvidenceMinimum = math.max(65.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 10.0)
bool bullishReversalTrigger = setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == 1 and setupCandidateState.reversalEligible
bool bearishReversalTrigger = setupCandidateActive and setupCandidateDirection == -1 and setupCandidateState.reversalEligible
bool bullishConfirmedReversalPass = resolvedStructureBias == 1 and bullishReversalTrigger and bullishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
bool bearishConfirmedReversalPass = resolvedStructureBias == -1 and bearishReversalTrigger and bearishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
bool weakHighLiquidity = resolvedStructureBias == 1 and not na(lastSwingHigh)
bool weakLowLiquidity = resolvedStructureBias == -1 and not na(lastSwingLow)
bool protectedLowSwing = resolvedStructureBias == 1 and not na(lastSwingLow)
bool protectedHighSwing = resolvedStructureBias == -1 and not na(lastSwingHigh)
bool analysisIntegrityPass = swingLevelsCompatible and not structureDirectionConflict
bool majorLongOpposed = majorTrendReady and (majorChannelSignal == -1 or (majorChannelSignal == 0 and majorChannelBias == -1))
bool majorShortOpposed = majorTrendReady and (majorChannelSignal == 1 or (majorChannelSignal == 0 and majorChannelBias == 1))
bool minorLongOpposed = trendReady and (channelSignal == -1 or (channelSignal == 0 and channelBias == -1))
bool minorShortOpposed = trendReady and (channelSignal == 1 or (channelSignal == 0 and channelBias == 1))
bool longTrendLayersPass = (not channelLayerConflict and not majorLongOpposed and not minorLongOpposed) or bullishConfirmedReversalPass
bool shortTrendLayersPass = (not channelLayerConflict and not majorShortOpposed and not minorShortOpposed) or bearishConfirmedReversalPass
bool higherLongAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherTrendBias != -1 or bullishConfirmedReversalPass
bool higherShortAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherTrendBias != 1 or bearishConfirmedReversalPass
int requestedPlanDirection = actionableLongSetup and analysisIntegrityPass and longTrendLayersPass and longEvidencePass and higherLongAlignmentPass and not actionableShortSetup ? 1 : actionableShortSetup and analysisIntegrityPass and shortTrendLayersPass and shortEvidencePass and higherShortAlignmentPass and not actionableLongSetup ? -1 : 0
bool requestedEarlyEntry = requestedPlanDirection == 1 ? bullishEarlyCloseQuality : requestedPlanDirection == -1 ? bearishEarlyCloseQuality : false
if planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and requestedPlanDirection != planDirection
    planActive := false
    planState := "إغلاق بإشارة عكسية"
    lastPlanResult := planState
    lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
    planOutcomeBar := bar_index
    planOutcomeKind := 4
    planOutcomePrice := close
    f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 72, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 82, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)

// جميع نهايات الخطة تمر من هنا: أوقف الخطوط، احذف تفاصيل الخطة، واحتفظ
// بعلامة نتيجة واحدة فقط. تتكرر الكتلة أثناء الشمعة الحية حتى تثبت الرسوم بعد الإغلاق.
bool planEndedThisBar = not planActive and not na(planOutcomeBar) and planOutcomeBar == bar_index
if planEndedThisBar
    entryLine := f_clear_line(entryLine)
    stopLine := f_clear_line(stopLine)
    target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
    target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
    target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
    planBox := f_clear_label(planBox)
    entryLevelLabel := f_clear_label(entryLevelLabel)
    stopLevelLabel := f_clear_label(stopLevelLabel)
    target1LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target1LevelLabel)
    target2LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target2LevelLabel)
    target3LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target3LevelLabel)
    activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
    activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
    activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
    activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
    planHighSinceEntry := na
    planLowSinceEntry := na
bool planCooldownComplete = na(lastPlanExitBar) or bar_index - lastPlanExitBar > planReentryCooldownBars
bool createNewPlan = barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and not planActive and not planEndedThisBar and planCooldownComplete
bool newPlanCreatedEvent = false

if createNewPlan
    bool isLong = requestedPlanDirection == 1
    bool newPlanUsesStructureBreakGuard = setupCandidateState.usesGuard
    float newPlanStructureBreakGuardLevel = setupCandidateState.guardLevel
    float setupReferenceLevel = setupCandidateState.ref
    float genericSwingRange = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) ? math.abs(lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow) : na
    float setupRawRange = setupCandidateState.rng
    string selectedSetupSource = setupCandidateState.source + (requestedEarlyEntry ? " | دخول مبكر مؤكد" : setupCandidateState.directBreak ? " | كسر مؤكد" : " | اختبار مؤكد") + (strongEntryCluster ? " | كلاستر دخول " + str.tostring(entryClusterCount) : "")
    float baseContextualStopAnchor = setupCandidateState.stop
    bool protectedContextStop = setupCandidateState.stopKind == 2
    float setupSpecificStopAnchor = setupCandidateState.stopKind == 1 ? setupCandidateState.stop : na
    float nearestFibStopLevel = isLong ? nearestFibSupportForStop : nearestFibResistanceForStop
    float minorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (channelReady ? channelLowerNow : channelBias == 1 ? channelBaseNow : na) : (channelReady ? channelUpperNow : channelBias == -1 ? channelBaseNow : na)
    float majorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelLowerNow : majorChannelBias == 1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na) : (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelBias == -1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na)
    float stopCandleLevel = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, isLong ? highVolumeSupportLow : highVolumeResistanceHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleLow : lastKeyCandleHigh, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float protectedSwingStop = isLong ? (protectedLowSwing ? lastSwingLow : na) : (protectedHighSwing ? lastSwingHigh : na)
    float weakSwingTarget = isLong ? (weakHighLiquidity ? lastSwingHigh : na) : (weakLowLiquidity ? lastSwingLow : na)
    float stopClusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.30, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float minimumStopAtrMultiple = chartSeconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 1.10 : chartSeconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 1.20 : chartSeconds <= 31 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 1.30 : 1.40
    float maximumStopAtrMultiple = chartSeconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 5.00 : chartSeconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 6.00 : chartSeconds <= 31 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 7.00 : 8.00
    float stopMaximumDistance = safeAtr * (maximumStopAtrMultiple + stopAtrBuffer)
    [rankedStopAnchor, rankedStopScore, rankedStopCount, rankedStopRank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, stopMaximumDistance, stopClusterTolerance, protectedSwingStop, isLong ? strongSupportLow : strongResistanceHigh, isLong ? demandZoneLow : supplyZoneHigh, isLong ? wyckoffRangeSupport : wyckoffRangeResistance, minorChannelStopLevel, majorChannelStopLevel, nearestFibStopLevel, ema20, ema50, stopCandleLevel)
    float contextualStopAnchor = protectedContextStop ? baseContextualStopAnchor : not na(setupSpecificStopAnchor) ? setupSpecificStopAnchor : nz(rankedStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor)
    string stopReferenceText = protectedContextStop ? " | وقف خلف الإبطال" : not na(setupSpecificStopAnchor) ? " | وقف خلف الإعداد" : rankedStopCount >= 2 ? " | وقف خلف كلاستر " + str.tostring(rankedStopCount) : rankedStopRank <= 3 ? " | وقف خلف قمة/قاع" : rankedStopRank <= 5 ? " | وقف خلف قناة" : rankedStopRank == 6 ? " | وقف خلف فيبوناتشي" : rankedStopRank <= 8 ? " | وقف خلف متوسط" : not na(rankedStopAnchor) ? " | وقف خلف شمعة حجم" : ""
    float validInvalidationAnchor = isLong ? math.min(contextualStopAnchor, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(contextualStopAnchor, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float rawStop = isLong ? validInvalidationAnchor - safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer : validInvalidationAnchor + safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer
    float rawRiskDistance = math.abs(close - rawStop)
    float minimumRiskDistance = useAdaptiveRiskGeometry ? safeAtr * minimumStopAtrMultiple : syminfo.mintick
    float volatilityAdjustedStop = rawRiskDistance < minimumRiskDistance ? (isLong ? close - minimumRiskDistance : close + minimumRiskDistance) : rawStop
    float validStopUnrounded = isLong ? math.min(volatilityAdjustedStop, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(volatilityAdjustedStop, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float validStop = isLong ? f_floor_to_tick(validStopUnrounded) : f_ceil_to_tick(validStopUnrounded)
    float risk = math.max(math.abs(close - validStop), syminfo.mintick)
    float swingRange = not na(setupRawRange) and setupRawRange > syminfo.mintick ? math.max(setupRawRange, risk) : not na(genericSwingRange) ? math.max(genericSwingRange, risk) : risk * 2.0

    // Risk already scales with the chart's structure and ATR. Do not shrink
    // reward below 1R on fast charts. Larger intervals may widen the ladder.
    float rewardHorizonFactor = math.max(targetHorizonFactor, 1.0)
    float orderedRr1 = math.max(rr1 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget1RiskMultiple)
    float orderedRr2 = math.max(rr2 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr1 + 0.75)
    float orderedRr3 = math.max(rr3 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr2 + 1.00)
    float minimumTarget1Distance = risk * orderedRr1
    float minimumTarget2Distance = risk * orderedRr2
    float minimumTarget3Distance = risk * orderedRr3
    float maximumTarget1Distance = risk * 2.0 * rewardHorizonFactor
    float maximumTarget2Distance = risk * 4.0 * rewardHorizonFactor
    float maximumTarget3Distance = risk * 6.0 * rewardHorizonFactor
    float minimumTargetGap = risk * 0.50
    float candidateTarget1 = na
    float candidateTarget2 = na
    float candidateTarget3 = na
    float fibObjective1 = na
    float fibObjective2 = na
    float fibObjective3 = na
    if targetMode == RR_MODE
        candidateTarget1 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget1Distance : close - minimumTarget1Distance
        candidateTarget2 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget2Distance : close - minimumTarget2Distance
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget3Distance : close - minimumTarget3Distance
    else
        float brokenLevel = nz(setupReferenceLevel, isLong ? nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : nz(lastSwingLow, close))
        [fibTarget1, fibTarget2, fibTarget3, selectedFibLevel1, selectedFibLevel2, selectedFibLevel3] = f_build_fib_extension_targets(isLong, brokenLevel, swingRange, close, minimumTarget1Distance, minimumTarget2Distance, minimumTarget3Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget2Distance, maximumTarget3Distance, minimumTargetGap)
        candidateTarget1 := fibTarget1
        candidateTarget2 := fibTarget2
        candidateTarget3 := fibTarget3
        fibObjective1 := selectedFibLevel1
        fibObjective2 := selectedFibLevel2
        fibObjective3 := selectedFibLevel3

    float measuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMeasuredTarget : bearishMeasuredTarget
    if not na(measuredTarget) and (isLong ? measuredTarget > close : measuredTarget < close)
        // A measured objective may extend T3, but never beyond the adaptive
        // maximum reward envelope for this chart interval.
        float boundedMeasuredTarget = isLong ? math.min(measuredTarget, close + maximumTarget3Distance) : math.max(measuredTarget, close - maximumTarget3Distance)
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? math.max(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget) : math.min(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget)

    // محرك الأهداف: العائق البنيوي الأقرب لا يُقفز، ثم تُرتب المستويات حسب
    // القمم/القيعان، القنوات، فيبوناتشي، المتوسطات، ثم شموع الحجم.
    float minimumStructuralTargetDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    float retracementTargetLevel = isLong ? nearestFibResistanceForTarget : nearestFibSupportForTarget
    float nearestFibTargetLevel1 = targetMode == FIB_MODE ? fibObjective1 : retracementTargetLevel
    float nearestFibTargetLevel2 = targetMode == FIB_MODE ? fibObjective2 : retracementTargetLevel
    float nearestFibTargetLevel3 = targetMode == FIB_MODE ? fibObjective3 : retracementTargetLevel
    float minorChannelBoundaryTarget = isLong ? channelUpperNow : channelLowerNow
    float majorChannelBoundaryTarget = isLong ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerNow
    float horizontalObstacleLevel = isLong ? (strongResistanceReady and not strongResistanceRoleReversed ? strongResistanceLow : na) : (strongSupportReady and not strongSupportRoleReversed ? strongSupportHigh : na)
    float opposingZoneBoundary = isLong ? f_nearest_price_level(true, close, syminfo.mintick, supplyZoneLow, bearishBreakerLow, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na) : f_nearest_price_level(false, close, syminfo.mintick, demandZoneHigh, bullishBreakerHigh, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float targetWyckoffLevel = isLong ? wyckoffRangeResistance : wyckoffRangeSupport
    float targetCandleLevel = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, isLong ? highVolumeResistanceLow : highVolumeSupportHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleHigh : lastKeyCandleLow, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    bool insideOpposingSupplyDemand = isLong ? supplyZoneActive and close >= supplyZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= supplyZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick : demandZoneActive and close >= demandZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= demandZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick
    bool insideOpposingHorizontalZone = isLong ? strongResistanceReady and not resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and close >= strongResistanceLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= strongResistanceHigh + syminfo.mintick : strongSupportReady and not supportClusterActsAsResistance and close >= strongSupportLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= strongSupportHigh + syminfo.mintick
    bool insideOpposingHardZone = insideOpposingSupplyDemand or insideOpposingHorizontalZone
    float nearestHardObstacle = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, weakSwingTarget, horizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, minorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, targetWyckoffLevel, na, na, na, na)
    float hardObstacleDistanceR = not na(nearestHardObstacle) ? math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) / risk : na
    float requiredObstacleRoomR = math.max(minimumObstacleRoomRiskMultiple, orderedRr1)
    bool roomToObstacleValid = not useAdaptiveRiskGeometry or (not insideOpposingHardZone and (na(hardObstacleDistanceR) or hardObstacleDistanceR >= requiredObstacleRoomR))
    float clusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.30, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    [rankedTarget1, rankedTarget1Score, rankedTarget1Count, rankedTarget1Rank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(isLong, close, minimumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, clusterTolerance, weakSwingTarget, horizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, minorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, nearestFibTargetLevel1, ema20, ema50, targetCandleLevel)
    bool firstTargetUsesHardObstacle = not na(nearestHardObstacle) and (isLong ? nearestHardObstacle < nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1) : nearestHardObstacle > nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1)) and math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) >= minimumTarget1Distance
    float firstTargetCandidate = firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? nearestHardObstacle : rankedTarget1
    if not na(firstTargetCandidate)
        candidateTarget1 := firstTargetCandidate
    float target2MinimumDistance = math.max(minimumTarget2Distance, math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    [rankedTarget2, rankedTarget2Score, rankedTarget2Count, rankedTarget2Rank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(isLong, close, target2MinimumDistance, maximumTarget2Distance, clusterTolerance, weakSwingTarget, horizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, minorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, nearestFibTargetLevel2, ema20, ema50, targetCandleLevel)
    if not na(rankedTarget2)
        candidateTarget2 := rankedTarget2
    float target3MinimumDistance = math.max(minimumTarget3Distance, math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    [rankedTarget3, rankedTarget3Score, rankedTarget3Count, rankedTarget3Rank] = f_ranked_cluster_level(isLong, close, target3MinimumDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, clusterTolerance, weakSwingTarget, horizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, minorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, nearestFibTargetLevel3, ema20, ema50, targetCandleLevel)
    if not na(rankedTarget3)
        candidateTarget3 := rankedTarget3
    candidateTarget1 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget1) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget1)
    candidateTarget2 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget2) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget2)
    candidateTarget3 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget3) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget3)
    bool structuralTargetUsed = not na(firstTargetCandidate) or not na(rankedTarget2) or not na(rankedTarget3)
    int selectedTargetRank = firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? 0 : not na(firstTargetCandidate) ? rankedTarget1Rank : not na(rankedTarget2) ? rankedTarget2Rank : rankedTarget3Rank
    int selectedTargetClusterCount = math.max(firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? 1 : rankedTarget1Count, math.max(rankedTarget2Count, rankedTarget3Count))
    bool structuralTargetUsesFib = structuralTargetUsed and selectedTargetRank == 6
    bool structuralTargetUsesChannel = structuralTargetUsed and (selectedTargetRank == 4 or selectedTargetRank == 5)
    bool structuralTargetUsesHigherLevel = false
    string targetReferenceText = selectedTargetClusterCount >= 2 ? " | أهداف كلاستر " + str.tostring(selectedTargetClusterCount) : selectedTargetRank <= 3 ? " | أهداف قمم/قيعان" : selectedTargetRank <= 5 ? " | أهداف قناة" : selectedTargetRank == 6 ? " | أهداف فيبوناتشي" : selectedTargetRank <= 8 ? " | أهداف متوسطات" : structuralTargetUsed ? " | أهداف شموع حجم" : ""
    float planRiskPercent = close > 0 ? risk / close * 100.0 : 1000.0
    float planRiskAtrMultiple = safeAtr > 0 ? risk / safeAtr : 1000.0
    float adaptiveRiskPercentLimit = chartSeconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 20.0 : chartSeconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 25.0 : chartSeconds <= 31 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 35.0 : 45.0
    float effectiveRiskPercentLimit = useAdaptiveRiskGeometry ? math.min(maxPlanRiskPercent, adaptiveRiskPercentLimit) : maxPlanRiskPercent
    bool riskPercentValid = planRiskPercent <= effectiveRiskPercentLimit
    bool riskAtrValid = not useAdaptiveRiskGeometry or planRiskAtrMultiple <= maximumStopAtrMultiple + 0.0001
    bool riskDistanceValid = riskPercentValid and riskAtrValid
    bool stopPriceValid = validStop >= syminfo.mintick and (isLong ? validStop < close : validStop > close)
    bool planNumbersReady = not na(close) and not na(validStop) and not na(risk) and not na(candidateTarget1) and not na(candidateTarget2) and not na(candidateTarget3)
    bool targetPricesPositive = planNumbersReady and candidateTarget1 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget2 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget3 >= syminfo.mintick
    bool targetOrderValid = planNumbersReady and (isLong ? candidateTarget1 > close and candidateTarget2 > candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 > candidateTarget2 : candidateTarget1 < close and candidateTarget2 < candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 < candidateTarget2)
    float target1RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) / risk : na
    float target2RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) / risk : na
    float target3RiskMultiple = planNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget3 - close) / risk : na
    bool targetRewardValid = planNumbersReady and target1RiskMultiple + 0.0001 >= orderedRr1 and target2RiskMultiple >= target1RiskMultiple + 0.49 and target3RiskMultiple >= target2RiskMultiple + 0.49
    bool planGeometryValid = planNumbersReady and riskDistanceValid and roomToObstacleValid and stopPriceValid and targetPricesPositive and targetOrderValid and targetRewardValid

    if planGeometryValid
        int score = isLong ? bullishEvidence : bearishEvidence
        planDirection := isLong ? 1 : -1
        planActive := true
        planStartBar := bar_index
        planUsesStructureBreakGuard := newPlanUsesStructureBreakGuard
        planStructureBreakGuardLevel := newPlanStructureBreakGuardLevel
        planEntry := close
        planStop := validStop
        planTarget1 := candidateTarget1
        planTarget2 := candidateTarget2
        planTarget3 := candidateTarget3
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planInvalidationLevel := validInvalidationAnchor
        planScore := score
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := higherTrendBias != 0 and (isLong ? higherTrendBias == -1 : higherTrendBias == 1)
        planOutcomeBar := na
        planOutcomeKind := 0
        planOutcomePrice := na
        newPlanCreatedEvent := true
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na
        planTradeText := isLong ? "شراء" : "بيع"
        planPriceContext := priceContextText
        string structuralTargetSuffix = targetReferenceText
        planSetupSource := selectedSetupSource + stopReferenceText + structuralTargetSuffix
        planTargetHorizon := currentTargetHorizonText + " | وقف " + str.tostring(planRiskAtrMultiple, "#.##") + " ATR / " + str.tostring(planRiskPercent, "#.##") + "% | أهداف " + str.tostring(target1RiskMultiple, "#.##") + "R / " + str.tostring(target2RiskMultiple, "#.##") + "R / " + str.tostring(target3RiskMultiple, "#.##") + "R"
        planState := isLong ? "خطة شراء نشطة" : "خطة بيع نشطة"
        f_clear_setup_candidate(setupCandidateState)

        // هذه هي شمعة القرار الفعلية: لا تظهر العلامة إلا بعد إغلاق الشمعة
        // واكتمال الإعداد والأدلة وإدارة المخاطرة، لا لمجرد اقتراب السعر.

        entryLine := f_clear_line(entryLine)
        stopLine := f_clear_line(stopLine)
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
        target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
        target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
        planBox := f_clear_label(planBox)
        entryLevelLabel := f_clear_label(entryLevelLabel)
        stopLevelLabel := f_clear_label(stopLevelLabel)
        target1LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target1LevelLabel)
        target2LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target2LevelLabel)
        target3LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target3LevelLabel)
        activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
        activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
        activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
        activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
        color directionColor = isLong ? bullColor : bearColor
        // One fixed visual language for both rising and falling plans:
        // cyan entry, red stop, and dotted green targets.
        color confirmedEntryColor = isLong ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor
        entryLine := line.new(bar_index, planEntry, bar_index + 1, planEntry, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(confirmedEntryColor, planEntryLineTransparency), style = line.style_solid, width = 1)
        stopLine := line.new(bar_index, planStop, bar_index + 1, planStop, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#F23645, planStopLineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        target1Line := line.new(bar_index, planTarget1, bar_index + 1, planTarget1, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#22C55E, planTarget1LineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        target2Line := line.new(bar_index, planTarget2, bar_index + 1, planTarget2, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#22C55E, planTarget2LineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        target3Line := line.new(bar_index, planTarget3, bar_index + 1, planTarget3, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#22C55E, planTarget3LineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        int effectivePlanBoxOffset = visualExplanationOffsetBars
        float planSummaryPrice = (planTarget1 + planTarget2) * 0.5
        entryLevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planEntry, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = confirmedEntryColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        stopLevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planStop, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #F23645, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        target1LevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planTarget1, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #22C55E, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        target2LevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planTarget2, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #22C55E, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        target3LevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planTarget3, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #22C55E, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        planBox := showPlanBox ? label.new(bar_index + effectivePlanBoxOffset, planSummaryPrice, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(directionColor, 38), textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny) : na
    else
        planDirection := 0
        planActive := false
        planStartBar := na
        planEntry := na
        planStop := na
        planTarget1 := na
        planTarget2 := na
        planTarget3 := na
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planInvalidationLevel := na
        planScore := 0
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := false
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na
        planTradeText := "لا صفقة"
        planPriceContext := priceContextText
        planSetupSource := "مرفوضة بإدارة المخاطر"
        planTargetHorizon := currentTargetHorizonText
        planState := not riskPercentValid ? "رفض: الوقف " + str.tostring(planRiskPercent, "#.##") + "% أكبر من حد الفاصل" : not riskAtrValid ? "رفض: الوقف أبعد من " + str.tostring(maximumStopAtrMultiple, "#.##") + " ATR" : not roomToObstacleValid ? "رفض: عائق قريب أو منطقة معاكسة" : not stopPriceValid ? "رفض: وقف غير صالح" : not targetRewardValid ? "رفض: عائد/مخاطرة ضعيف" : "رفض: أهداف غير صالحة"
        lastPlanResult := planState

// عزل تحديث الرسومات في دالة يقلل حجم النطاق الرئيسي. الدالة لا تعيد
// إسناد مراجع عامة؛ هي تعدل خصائص line/label فقط، وهي أنواع مرجعية.
f_update_plan_level_label(label id, int x, float level, float offset, string caption, color textColor) =>
    label.set_xy(id, x, level + offset)
    label.set_text(id, caption + f_price(level))
    label.set_color(id, color.new(color.black, 100))
    label.set_textcolor(id, textColor)

f_refresh_plan_display() =>
    if not na(planBox)
        string warning = planCounterTrend ? "\nعكس إطار أعلى " + higherTimeframe : ""
        string trail = planTrailingStopActive ? " | وقف متحرك" : ""
        label.set_text(planBox, planTradeText + " | " + planState + trail + "\n" + planSetupSource + " | توافق " + str.tostring(planScore) + "/" + str.tostring(planScoreMax) + warning)
        label.set_y(planBox, (planTarget1 + planTarget2) * 0.5)
        label.set_x(planBox, planActive and barstate.islast ? bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars : bar_index)
    if not na(entryLevelLabel)
        int x = bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars
        float offset = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 3.0, safeAtr * 0.04)
        f_update_plan_level_label(entryLevelLabel, x, planEntry, offset, planDirection == 1 ? "دخول شراء " : "دخول بيع ", planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor)
        f_update_plan_level_label(stopLevelLabel, x, planStop, offset, "وقف ", #F23645)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target1LevelLabel, x, planTarget1, offset, "هدف 1" + (target1Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target2LevelLabel, x, planTarget2, offset, "هدف 2" + (target2Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target3LevelLabel, x, planTarget3, offset, "هدف 3" + (target3Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
    true
f_refresh_plan_display()

plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement236 : na, "فيبو 23.6%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 88), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement382 : na, "فيبو 38.2%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 80), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement500 : na, "فيبو 50%", color = color.new(neutralColor, 72), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement618 : na, "فيبو 61.8%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 78), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement786 : na, "فيبو 78.6%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 86), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars)

plot(stockChartAllowed and showVwap ? confirmedAnalysisVwapValue : na, "VWAP", color = color.new(#2962FF, 10), linewidth = 2)
// شمعة الدخول المؤكدة لها الأولوية البصرية حتى لو كان تلوين الاتجاه العام
// معطلا. بقية الشموع لا تتغير إلا إذا فعّل المستخدم تلوين الاتجاه.
barcolor(stockChartAllowed and newPlanCreatedEvent ? (planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor) : na)
// إشارات الشارت بقالب موحد؛ الاختلاف في النص واللون والاتجاه فقط.
int maximumUnifiedChartLabels = 18
bool watchStageMarker = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and formationDirection != 0 and not setupCandidateActive and not planActive and (showHistoricalOpportunityMarkers ? formationDirection != nz(formationDirection[1], 0) : barstate.islast)
bool candidateStageMarker = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and setupCandidateActive and not planActive and (showHistoricalOpportunityMarkers ? (not setupCandidateActive[1] or setupCandidateDirection != nz(setupCandidateDirection[1], 0)) : barstate.islast)
if showHistoricalOpportunityMarkers
    activeStageLabel := f_clear_label(activeStageLabel)
    if watchStageMarker
        f_push_chart_marker(chartEventLabels, maximumUnifiedChartLabels, bar_index, formationDirection == 1 ? low : high, "راقب", formationDirection == 1, formationDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.30, 0.18)
    else if candidateStageMarker
        f_push_chart_marker(chartEventLabels, maximumUnifiedChartLabels, bar_index, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? low : high, "مرشح", setupCandidateDirection == 1, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.38, 0.18)
else if barstate.islast
    activeStageLabel := f_clear_label(activeStageLabel)
    if watchStageMarker
        activeStageLabel := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, formationDirection == 1 ? low : high, "راقب", formationDirection == 1, formationDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.30)
    else if candidateStageMarker
        activeStageLabel := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? low : high, "مرشح", setupCandidateDirection == 1, setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, safeAtr, 0.38)

bool unifiedEventMarkerNow = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed and unifiedEventMarkerEnabled and not newPlanCreatedEvent
if unifiedEventMarkerNow
    f_push_chart_marker(chartEventLabels, maximumUnifiedChartLabels, unifiedEventBar, unifiedEventLevel, unifiedEventText, unifiedEventDirection >= 0, unifiedEventColor, unifiedEventAtr, 0.40, 0.18)

if stockChartAllowed
    if newPlanCreatedEvent
        activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
        activePlanEntryMarker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? low : high, planDirection == 1 ? "شراء" : "بيع", planDirection == 1, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, safeAtr, 0.25)
    if planActive
        if target1Done and target1HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
            activePlanTarget1Marker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planTarget1, "هدف 1", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.34)
        if target2Done and target2HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
            activePlanTarget2Marker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planTarget2, "هدف 2", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.34)
        if target3Done and target3HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
            activePlanTarget3Marker := f_new_chart_marker(bar_index, planTarget3, "هدف 3", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.34)

var table dashboard = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 8, bgcolor = color.new(#111827, 12), frame_color = color.new(neutralColor, 45), frame_width = 1, border_color = color.new(neutralColor, 78), border_width = 1)
if barstate.isfirst
    table.merge_cells(dashboard, 0, 0, 1, 0)

f_render_dashboard() =>
    if barstate.islast
        if showDashboard
            color labelBg = color.new(neutralColor, 68)
            color valueBg = color.new(#111827, 12)
            int displayedAdaptiveBias = barstate.isconfirmed ? adaptiveTrendBias : adaptiveTrendBias[1]
            float displayedVolumeZ = barstate.isconfirmed ? volumeZScore : volumeZScore[1]
            int displayedHeatmapTier = barstate.isconfirmed ? heatmapVolumeTier : heatmapVolumeTier[1]
            string displayedHeatmapText = f_text_code(TXT_HEATMAP_TIER, displayedHeatmapTier)
            string currentBias = closedStructureBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : closedStructureBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "تحول"
            string higherBias = closedHigherTrendBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : closedHigherTrendBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "تحول"
            string warningText = showContextWarningLabel ? closedStructureConflict ? "\nتعارض هيكلي" : closedStructureBias != 0 and closedHigherTrendBias != 0 and closedStructureBias != closedHigherTrendBias ? "\nاختلاف إطار أعلى" : "" : ""
            string directionText = currentBias + " | داو " + closedDowState + " | إطار أعلى " + higherBias + "\nتكيفي " + (displayedAdaptiveBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : displayedAdaptiveBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "محايد") + warningText
            string evidenceText = "شراء " + str.tostring(bullishEvidencePercent, "#.0") + "% | بيع " + str.tostring(bearishEvidencePercent, "#.0") + "% | حد " + str.tostring(minimumPlanEvidencePercent, "#") + "%\nحجم " + (closedVolumeAvailable ? f_ratio(closedRelativeVolume) : "—") + " | انحراف " + (na(displayedVolumeZ) ? "—" : str.tostring(displayedVolumeZ, "#.0")) + " | حرارة " + displayedHeatmapText
            string wyckoffText = closedWyckoffPhaseState + "\n" + closedWyckoffTrendContextState + "\n" + (showWyckoffModelLabels ? closedWyckoffEvent : "الحدث مخفي")
            string technicalText = closedTechnicalPatternState + (showImportantCandleLabel ? "\nشمعة: " + closedBehaviorState : "") + (showEventLabel ? "\nحدث: " + closedClassicEvent : "")
            string contextText = closedZoneState + "\n" + closedHorizontalSrState + "\n" + closedChannelState + " | فجوة " + (closedRecentBullFvg ? "صاعد" : closedRecentBearFvg ? "هابط" : "لا")
            string opportunityText = setupCandidateActive ? (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? "مرشح شراء" : "مرشح بيع") + " | " + setupCandidateState.source + "\nمتبقي " + str.tostring(math.max(0, setupCandidateMaximumBars - (bar_index - setupCandidateState.eventBar))) + " شمعة" : formationDirection != 0 ? (formationDirection == 1 ? "مراقبة شراء" : "مراقبة بيع") + " | " + formationSource : "لا فرصة"
            string planText = planActive ? (planDirection == 1 ? "شراء" : "بيع") + " | " + planSetupSource + "\n" + planState + " | توافق " + str.tostring(planScore) + "/" + str.tostring(planScoreMax) : opportunityText + "\nآخر: " + lastPlanResult
            string riskText = planActive ? "دخول " + f_price(planEntry) + " | وقف " + f_price(planStop) + "\nهدف 1 " + f_price(planTarget1) + (target1Done ? " ✓" : "") + " | هدف 2 " + f_price(planTarget2) + (target2Done ? " ✓" : "") + "\nهدف 3 " + f_price(planTarget3) + (target3Done ? " ✓" : "") : "لا خطة"
            color structureColor = closedStructureConflict ? warningColor : closedStructureBias == 1 ? bullColor : closedStructureBias == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
            color planColor = planActive ? (planDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor) : setupCandidateActive ? (setupCandidateDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor) : formationDirection == 1 ? bullColor : formationDirection == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
            table.cell(dashboard, 0, 0, "ملخص التحليل SC-V72-D", text_color = color.white, bgcolor = color.new(neutralColor, 35), text_size = 8, text_halign = text.align_center)
            array<string> labels = array.from("الاتجاه", "التوافق", "وايكوف", "الفني", "السياق", "الخطة", "الإدارة")
            array<string> values = array.from(directionText, evidenceText, wyckoffText, technicalText, contextText, planText, riskText)
            array<color> colors = array.from(structureColor, color.white, color.white, color.white, neutralColor, planColor, planColor)
            for row = 0 to 6
                table.cell(dashboard, 0, row + 1, array.get(labels, row), width = 8, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBg, text_size = 8, text_halign = text.align_right)
                table.cell(dashboard, 1, row + 1, array.get(values, row), width = 36, text_color = array.get(colors, row), bgcolor = valueBg, text_size = row >= 5 ? 8 : 7, text_halign = text.align_right)
        else
            table.clear(dashboard, 0, 0, 1, 7)
    true
f_render_dashboard()
